1	# clui — Design Specification
2	
3	> TUI wrapper around Claude Code with multi-session tabs, skills management, and context tools.
4	
5	## Overview
6	
7	clui is a terminal user interface that wraps the Claude Code SDK (`@anthropic-ai/claude-code`) to provide a richer development experience. It adds multi-session tabs with process isolation, a session history sidebar, a three-tier skills management sidebar, context management tools (pins, snapshots, compact indicator, reset), configurable status bar, and optional git worktree isolation per tab.
8	
9	## Architecture
10	
11	### Shell + Workers Model
12	
13	The app runs as two layers:
14	
15	- **Shell process** — the main Ink/React TUI. Renders the three-column layout, tab bar, sidebars, input, status bar. Manages UI state via Zustand. Spawns and supervises worker processes.
16	- **Worker processes** — one per tab/session. Each worker runs a Claude Code SDK instance in isolation. Communicates with the shell via IPC (Unix Domain Sockets). Manages its own context, pins, and optional git worktree.
17	
18	```
19	Shell (Ink/React)
20	  ├── UI Components (Layout, TabBar, Sidebars, ChatView, InputBar, StatusBar)
21	  ├── Zustand Store (tabs, sessions, skills, settings)
22	  ├── IPC Server (Unix Domain Socket)
23	  │
24	  ├── Worker 1 (Claude SDK + context manager + worktree manager)
25	  ├── Worker 2 (Claude SDK + context manager + worktree manager)
26	  └── Worker N ...
27	  │
28	  └── SQLite Database (shared: sessions, messages, snapshots, pins, settings)
29	```
30	
31	### Why Shell + Workers
32	
33	- **Isolation**: One worker crash does not kill the shell or other tabs.
34	- **Resource containment**: Each Claude SDK instance runs in its own process with its own memory.
35	- **Cross-tab opt-in**: Workers are isolated by default. Cross-tab context reading is an explicit IPC request, not shared state.
36	
37	## Tech Stack
38	
39	| Concern         | Choice                          | Rationale                                           |
40	|-----------------|---------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------|
41	| Language        | TypeScript                      | User preference, matches existing codebase          |
42	| TUI Framework   | Ink (React for CLI)             | Component model, hooks, familiar React patterns     |
43	| Agent SDK       | `@anthropic-ai/claude-code`     | Programmatic control, structured responses. API shape must be verified during Phase 1 — fallback to subprocess spawning if SDK doesn't expose needed events (streaming, compact notifications). |
44	| UI State        | Zustand                         | Lightweight, works with React, no boilerplate       |
45	| Database        | SQLite via `better-sqlite3`     | Single file, no server, good for local persistence  |
46	| IPC             | Unix Domain Sockets (`node:net`)| Fast, local-only, reliable                          |
47	| Git             | `simple-git`                    | Worktree management, diff stats, branch info        |
48	| Build           | tsup                            | Bundle to single distributable                      |
49	
50	## Layout
51	
52	Three-column layout with tab bar at top, context bar + input + status bar at bottom of center column.
53	
54	```
55	┌──────────┬──────────────────────────────────┬──────────────┐
56	│ Sessions │  [Tab1: fix-auth] [Tab2: refactor] [+]         │ Skills       │
57	│          ├──────────────────────────────────┤              │
58	│ ● fix-auth│                                │ ▼ Repo       │
59	│   refactor│  Chat output (streaming)       │   ☑ tdd      │
60	│   chat-3  │                                │   ☑ nestjs   │
61	│          │                                  │   ☐ db       │
62	│          │                                  │ ▶ User       │
63	│          │                                  │ ▶ Global     │
64	│          ├──────────────────────────────────┤              │
65	│          │ 📌 src/auth.ts | 📌 tdd (sticky)│              │
66	│          │ 1️⃣ nestjs-skill | /commit        │              │
67	│          ├──────────────────────────────────┤              │
68	│          │ > _                              │              │
69	│          ├──────────────────────────────────┤              │
70	│          │ Opus 4.6 | Ctx: 31k/1M (3%) | $0.31 | main    │              │
71	└──────────┴──────────────────────────────────┴──────────────┘
72	```
73	
74	## Components
75	
76	### 1. Session Sidebar (Left)
77	
78	- **Data source**: Claude Code session API (reuse existing session data).
79	- **Display**: List of sessions with name, date, cost, duration.
80	- **Color tags**: User assigns a color dot to any session. Stored in SQLite.
81	- **Rename**: Inline rename (select + type). Stored in SQLite as override.
82	- **Search**: Filter sessions by name/date.
83	- **Actions**: Click to open in new tab. Right-arrow or Enter to resume.
84	- **Ordering**: Most recent first.
85	
86	### 2. Tab Bar (Top of center column)
87	
88	- **Tabs represent sessions**. Each tab = one worker process.
89	- **Naming**: Auto-indexed (`1`, `2`, `3`...) + session name (from task, user-given, or auto).
90	- **Creation**: `[+]` button or hotkey.
91	- **Switching**: Click tab or hotkey (e.g., Ctrl+1..9, Ctrl+Tab).
92	- **Closing**: Close button on tab.
93	  - No worktree → close immediately, worker killed.
94	  - Has worktree → prompt: "Close and keep worktree" or "Close and clean up worktree".
95	- **No tab limit**.
96	
97	### 3. Skills Sidebar (Right)
98	
99	Three collapsible tiers with cascade priority:
100	
101	1. **Repo** (open by default) — skills from `.claude/skills/` in current repo. Highest priority.
102	2. **User** (collapsed) — skills from `~/.claude/skills/`. Overridden by repo.
103	3. **Global** (collapsed) — bundled/system skills. Overridden by both.
104	
105	**Visual rules**:
106	- Active skills: normal text, full color.
107	- Overridden skills (same name exists in higher tier): greyed out, non-interactive.
108	- Each skill has a pin button next to it.
109	
110	**Pin interaction**:
111	- Click pin → add to context bar as one-shot (used for next prompt only).
112	- Modifier+click (e.g., Shift+click) → add as sticky (persists across prompts).
113	- Same interaction pattern for files and commands.
114	
115	### 4. Context Bar (Above Input)
116	
117	Unified view of everything attached to the current/next prompt:
118	
119	- **Sticky items** (📌): Pinned files and skills that persist across all prompts in this session.
120	- **One-shot items** (1️⃣): Files, skills, commands attached for the next prompt only. Auto-removed after send.
121	- **Selected capabilities**: Any skill or command selected from the sidebar.
122	- **Click to remove** any item.
123	- **Quick-add in input**: Type `@filepath` to pin a file, `/skill-name` to pin a skill.
124	
125	### 5. Input Bar
126	
127	- Text input with multiline support.
128	- Sends to active tab's worker via IPC.
129	- Context bar items are prepended to the prompt as system context.
130	
131	### 6. Status Bar (Below Input)
132	
133	- **User-configurable**: Settings UI to toggle which fields to show.
134	- **Available fields**:
135	  - Model name and context window size
136	  - Total tokens used in session
137	  - Current context usage (absolute + percentage)
138	  - Style/persona name
139	  - Session ID
140	  - Session duration (live timer)
141	  - Session cost
142	  - Git branch name
143	  - Git diff stats (+added, -removed, ~modified)
144	- **Auto-compact indicator**: Icon/flash in status bar when context compression occurs. Expandable to show what was dropped.
145	
146	## Context Management
147	
148	### Pin System
149	
150	Two modes:
151	- **Per-prompt (one-shot)**: Attached to the next prompt only. Removed after sending.
152	- **Sticky**: Persists across all prompts in the session until manually removed.
153	
154	Applies to:
155	- Files (read and included in context)
156	- Skills (loaded as system instructions)
157	- Commands (slash commands)
158	
159	Storage: Pins stored in SQLite `pins` table, linked to session.
160	
161	### Conversation Snapshot
162	
163	- **Trigger**: Hotkey or command.
164	- **Action**: Summarize current conversation using Claude, save summary to a memory file (markdown in `.claude/memory/` or project memory dir).
165	- **Purpose**: Reusable context in future sessions. Captures key decisions, findings, and state.
166	- **Storage**: Snapshot metadata in SQLite `snapshots` table. Actual summary in markdown file.
167	
168	### Auto-Compact Indicator
169	
170	- **Detection**: Monitor Claude Code SDK events for context compression.
171	- **Display**: Icon in status bar changes state (e.g., normal → compressed).
172	- **Detail view**: Expandable panel showing what was dropped/compressed and when.
173	
174	### Context Reset
175	
176	- **Trigger**: Hotkey or command.
177	- **Action**: Clear the Claude SDK context for the active tab's worker. Session history remains in SQLite.
178	- **Confirmation**: Prompt before reset.
179	
180	### Cross-Tab Context Reading
181	
182	- **Opt-in**: Explicit command or hotkey to request context from another tab.
183	- **Mechanism**: IPC message from requesting worker → shell → target worker. Target worker returns a context summary (not full context).
184	- **Use case**: "What did tab 2 learn about the auth module?" without copy-pasting.
185	
186	## Tabs & Worktrees
187	
188	### Tab Lifecycle
189	
190	1. User clicks [+] or hotkey.
191	2. Shell spawns a new worker process.
192	3. Worker initializes Claude Code SDK session.
193	4. Tab appears in tab bar with index + name.
194	5. User can optionally attach a git worktree.
195	
196	### Worktree Management
197	
198	- **Creation**: User command/UI to create worktree for active tab.
199	- **Isolation**: Each worktree is a separate git working directory.
200	- **Cleanup on tab close**:
201	  - No worktree: worker killed, tab removed.
202	  - Has worktree: prompt user — keep or cleanup (`git worktree remove`).
203	- **Branch**: Worktree branch shown in status bar.
204	
205	## Data Model (SQLite)
206	
207	### sessions
208	| Column           | Type    | Description                        |
209	|------------------|---------|------------------------------------|
210	| id               | TEXT PK | UUID                               |
211	| claude_session_id| TEXT    | Claude Code's own session ID       |
212	| name             | TEXT    | Display name (user-given or auto)  |
213	| color            | TEXT    | Hex color for session tag          |
214	| repo_path        | TEXT    | Project directory                  |
215	| worktree_path    | TEXT    | Git worktree path (nullable)       |
216	| worktree_branch  | TEXT    | Branch name (nullable)             |
217	| model            | TEXT    | Model used                         |
218	| total_tokens     | INTEGER | Cumulative tokens                  |
219	| total_cost       | REAL    | Cumulative cost in USD             |
220	| duration_seconds | INTEGER | Session duration                   |
221	| status           | TEXT    | active / closed / archived         |
222	| created_at       | INTEGER | Unix timestamp                     |
223	| updated_at       | INTEGER | Unix timestamp                     |
224	
225	### messages
226	| Column     | Type    | Description                      |
227	|------------|---------|----------------------------------|
228	| id         | TEXT PK | UUID                             |
229	| session_id | TEXT FK | References sessions.id           |
230	| role       | TEXT    | user / assistant / system / tool |
231	| content    | TEXT    | Message content                  |
232	| tokens     | INTEGER | Token count for this message     |
233	| cost       | REAL    | Cost for this message            |
234	| created_at | INTEGER | Unix timestamp                   |
235	
236	### snapshots
237	| Column        | Type    | Description                     |
238	|---------------|---------|---------------------------------|
239	| id            | TEXT PK | UUID                            |
240	| session_id    | TEXT FK | References sessions.id          |
241	| summary       | TEXT    | AI-generated conversation summary|
242	| context_state | TEXT    | JSON: active pins, skills, etc. |
243	| file_path     | TEXT    | Path to saved memory file       |
244	| created_at    | INTEGER | Unix timestamp                  |
245	
246	### pins
247	| Column     | Type    | Description                    |
248	|------------|---------|--------------------------------|
249	| id         | TEXT PK | UUID                           |
250	| session_id | TEXT FK | References sessions.id         |
251	| type       | TEXT    | file / skill / command         |
252	| target     | TEXT    | File path or skill/command name|
253	| mode       | TEXT    | prompt (one-shot) / sticky     |
254	| created_at | INTEGER | Unix timestamp                 |
255	
256	### settings
257	| Column | Type    | Description        |
258	|--------|---------|--------------------|
259	| key    | TEXT PK | Setting identifier |
260	| value  | TEXT    | JSON value         |
261	
262	## IPC Protocol
263	
264	Communication between shell and workers uses JSON messages over Unix Domain Sockets.
265	
266	### Message Types
267	
268	**Shell → Worker:**
269	- `send_prompt` — user input + context (pins, skills)
270	- `context_reset` — clear context
271	- `context_snapshot` — request conversation summary
272	- `get_context_summary` — cross-tab context read request
273	- `set_worktree` — attach/detach worktree
274	- `shutdown` — graceful worker shutdown
275	
276	**Worker → Shell:**
277	- `stream_chunk` — streaming assistant output
278	- `message_complete` — full message with token/cost data
279	- `context_compacted` — auto-compact event with details
280	- `context_summary` — response to cross-tab read
281	- `status_update` — model, tokens, cost, context usage
282	- `error` — worker error
283	- `ready` — worker initialized
284	
285	## Project Structure
286

288	clui/
289	├── package.json
290	├── tsconfig.json

292	│   ├── index.tsx                  # Entry point — parse args, launch shell

306	│   │   ├── store/

308	│   │   │   ├── tabs.ts               # Tab state & actions

343	
344	## Implementation Phases
345	
346	### Phase 1 — Foundation
347	Project scaffold (package.json, tsconfig, tsup). Three-column Ink layout with placeholder panels. Single tab with Claude Code SDK integration. Basic chat input/output with streaming. Status bar with hardcoded fields.
348	
349	### Phase 2 — Multi-Tab & Workers
350	Worker process spawning via `child_process.fork()`. IPC server/client over Unix Domain Sockets. Tab creation, switching, closing. Zustand store for tab state. SQLite setup with sessions and messages tables.
351	
352	### Phase 3 — Session History
353	Session sidebar component. Integration with Claude Code session API to list past sessions. Color tag assignment and inline rename. Session search/filter. Click to open session in new tab.
354	
355	### Phase 4 — Skills & Context Bar
356	Skills sidebar with three-tier discovery (repo → user → global). Collapse/expand tiers. Grey out overridden skills. Context bar above input showing all active items (pins, skills, commands). Pin interactions: click for one-shot, modifier+click for sticky. Quick-add via `@file` and `/skill` in input.
357	
358	### Phase 5 — Context Management
359	Conversation snapshot: summarize & save to memory file. Auto-compact indicator in status bar with expandable detail. Context reset with confirmation. Cross-tab context reading via IPC.
360	
361	### Phase 6 — Worktrees & Git
362	Worktree creation/attachment per tab. Git branch + diff stats in status bar. Worktree cleanup prompts on tab close. `simple-git` integration.
363	
364	## Hotkeys
365	
366	| Key              | Action                        |
367	|------------------|-------------------------------|
368	| Ctrl+T           | New tab                       |
369	| Ctrl+W           | Close tab                     |
370	| Ctrl+Tab         | Next tab                      |
371	| Ctrl+Shift+Tab   | Previous tab                  |
372	| Ctrl+1..9        | Switch to tab N               |
373	| Ctrl+H           | Toggle session sidebar        |
374	| Ctrl+S           | Toggle skills sidebar         |
375	| Ctrl+P           | Quick pin file (fuzzy finder) |
376	| Ctrl+R           | Context reset                 |
377	| Ctrl+Shift+S     | Conversation snapshot         |
378	| Ctrl+K           | Cross-tab context read        |
379

1	# clui Implementation Plan
2	
3	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
4	
5	**Goal:** Build a TUI wrapper around Claude Code (via `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk`) with multi-session tabs, process isolation, skills management, context tools, and git worktree support.
6	
7	**Architecture:** Shell + Workers model. The shell is an Ink/React TUI (main process) that renders the three-column layout and manages UI state via Zustand. Each tab spawns an isolated worker process that runs its own Claude Agent SDK instance. Shell and workers communicate over Unix Domain Sockets (JSON protocol). SQLite stores sessions, messages, pins, snapshots, and settings.
8	
9	**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, Ink 6 (React 19), `@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk`, Zustand, better-sqlite3, simple-git, tsup, ink-tab, ink-scroll-view, ink-text-input
10

385	```sql
386	-- db/migrations/001_init.sql
387	
388	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions (
389	  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
390	  claude_session_id TEXT,
391	  name TEXT NOT NULL,
392	  color TEXT,
393	  repo_path TEXT,
394	  worktree_path TEXT,
395	  worktree_branch TEXT,
396	  model TEXT,
397	  total_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
398	  total_cost REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
399	  duration_seconds INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
400	  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
401	  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
402	  updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
403	);
404	
405	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
406	  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
407	  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
408	  role TEXT NOT NULL,
409	  content TEXT NOT NULL,
410	  tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
411	  cost REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
412	  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
413	);
414	
415	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_messages_session ON messages(session_id, created_at);
416	
417	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS snapshots (
418	  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
419	  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
420	  summary TEXT NOT NULL,
421	  context_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
422	  file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
423	  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
424	);
425	
426	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pins (
427	  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
428	  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
429	  type TEXT NOT NULL,
430	  target TEXT NOT NULL,
431	  mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'prompt',
432	  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
433	);
434	
435	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pins_session ON pins(session_id);
436	
437	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (
438	  key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
439	  value TEXT NOT NULL
440	);
441	```

443	- [ ] **Step 2: Write the database module**
444	
445	```typescript
446	// src/shared/db.ts
447	
448	import Database from "better-sqlite3";
449	import fs from "node:fs";
450	import path from "node:path";
451	import { config, ensureDirs } from "./config.js";
452	import type { Session, Message, Pin, Snapshot } from "./types.js";
453	
454	let db: Database.Database | null = null;
455	
456	export function getDb(): Database.Database {
457	  if (db) return db;
458	
459	  ensureDirs();
460	  db = new Database(config.dbPath);
461	  db.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
462	  db.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");
463	  runMigrations(db);
464	  return db;
465	}
466	
467	function runMigrations(database: Database.Database): void {
468	  const migrationsDir = path.join(import.meta.dirname, "../../db/migrations");
469	  const files = fs.readdirSync(migrationsDir).sort();
470	
471	  database.exec(`
472	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _migrations (
473	      name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
474	      applied_at INTEGER NOT NULL
475	    )
476	  `);
477	
478	  const applied = new Set(
479	    database
480	      .prepare("SELECT name FROM _migrations")
481	      .all()
482	      .map((row: any) => row.name)
483	  );
484	
485	  for (const file of files) {
486	    if (applied.has(file)) continue;
487	    const sql = fs.readFileSync(path.join(migrationsDir, file), "utf-8");
488	    database.exec(sql);
489	    database
490	      .prepare("INSERT INTO _migrations (name, applied_at) VALUES (?, ?)")
491	      .run(file, Date.now());
492	  }
493	}
494	
495	// --- Sessions ---
496	
497	export function createSession(session: Session): void {
498	  getDb()
499	    .prepare(
```

500	      `INSERT INTO sessions (id, claude_session_id, name, color, repo_path, worktree_path, worktree_branch, model, total_tokens, total_cost, duration_seconds, status, created_at, updated_at)
501	       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
502	    )
503	    .run(
504	      session.id,
505	      session.claudeSessionId,
506	      session.name,
507	      session.color,
508	      session.repoPath,
509	      session.worktreePath,
510	      session.worktreeBranch,
511	      session.model,
512	      session.totalTokens,
513	      session.totalCost,
514	      session.durationSeconds,
515	      session.status,
516	      session.createdAt,
517	      session.updatedAt
518	    );
519	}
520	
521	export function updateSession(
522	  id: string,
523	  updates: Partial<
524	    Pick<Session, "name" | "color" | "status" | "totalTokens" | "totalCost" | "durationSeconds" | "claudeSessionId" | "worktreePath" | "worktreeBranch" | "model">
525	  >
526	): void {
527	  const fields: string[] = [];
528	  const values: any[] = [];
529	
530	  const columnMap: Record<string, string> = {
531	    name: "name",
532	    color: "color",
533	    status: "status",
534	    totalTokens: "total_tokens",
535	    totalCost: "total_cost",
536	    durationSeconds: "duration_seconds",
537	    claudeSessionId: "claude_session_id",
538	    worktreePath: "worktree_path",
539	    worktreeBranch: "worktree_branch",
540	    model: "model",
541	  };
542	
543	  for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(updates)) {
544	    const col = columnMap[key];
545	    if (col && val !== undefined) {
546	      fields.push(`${col} = ?`);
547	      values.push(val);
548	    }
549	  }
550	
551	  if (fields.length === 0) return;
552	
553	  fields.push("updated_at = ?");
554	  values.push(Date.now());
555	  values.push(id);
556	
557	  getDb()
558	    .prepare(`UPDATE sessions SET ${fields.join(", ")} WHERE id = ?`)
559	    .run(...values);
560	}
561	
562	export function listSessions(limit = 50): Session[] {
563	  return getDb()
564	    .prepare(
565	      `SELECT id, claude_session_id as claudeSessionId, name, color, repo_path as repoPath,
566	              worktree_path as worktreePath, worktree_branch as worktreeBranch, model,
567	              total_tokens as totalTokens, total_cost as totalCost,
568	              duration_seconds as durationSeconds, status, created_at as createdAt,
569	              updated_at as updatedAt
570	       FROM sessions ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT ?`
571	    )
572	    .all(limit) as Session[];
573	}
574	
575	export function getSession(id: string): Session | undefined {
576	  return getDb()
577	    .prepare(
578	      `SELECT id, claude_session_id as claudeSessionId, name, color, repo_path as repoPath,
579	              worktree_path as worktreePath, worktree_branch as worktreeBranch, model,
580	              total_tokens as totalTokens, total_cost as totalCost,
581	              duration_seconds as durationSeconds, status, created_at as createdAt,
582	              updated_at as updatedAt
583	       FROM sessions WHERE id = ?`
584	    )
585	    .get(id) as Session | undefined;
586	}
587	
588	// --- Messages ---
589	
590	export function addMessage(msg: Message): void {
591	  getDb()
592	    .prepare(
593	      `INSERT INTO messages (id, session_id, role, content, tokens, cost, created_at)
594	       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
595	    )
596	    .run(msg.id, msg.sessionId, msg.role, msg.content, msg.tokens, msg.cost, msg.createdAt);
597	}
598	
599	export function getMessages(sessionId: string, limit = 100, offset = 0): Message[] {
600	  return getDb()
601	    .prepare(
602	      `SELECT id, session_id as sessionId, role, content, tokens, cost, created_at as createdAt
603	       FROM messages WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`
604	    )
605	    .all(sessionId, limit, offset) as Message[];
606	}
607	
608	// --- Pins ---
609	
610	export function addPin(pin: Pin): void {
611	  getDb()
612	    .prepare(
613	      `INSERT INTO pins (id, session_id, type, target, mode, created_at)
614	       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
615	    )
616	    .run(pin.id, pin.sessionId, pin.type, pin.target, pin.mode, pin.createdAt);
617	}
618	
619	export function removePinById(id: string): void {
620	  getDb().prepare("DELETE FROM pins WHERE id = ?").run(id);
621	}
622	
623	export function getPins(sessionId: string): Pin[] {
624	  return getDb()
625	    .prepare(
626	      `SELECT id, session_id as sessionId, type, target, mode, created_at as createdAt
627	       FROM pins WHERE session_id = ?`
628	    )
629	    .all(sessionId) as Pin[];
630	}
631	
632	export function clearOneShots(sessionId: string): void {
633	  getDb()
634	    .prepare("DELETE FROM pins WHERE session_id = ? AND mode = 'prompt'")
635	    .run(sessionId);
636	}
637	
638	// --- Snapshots ---
639	
640	export function addSnapshot(snap: Snapshot): void {
641	  getDb()
642	    .prepare(
643	      `INSERT INTO snapshots (id, session_id, summary, context_state, file_path, created_at)
644	       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
645	    )
646	    .run(snap.id, snap.sessionId, snap.summary, snap.contextState, snap.filePath, snap.createdAt);
647	}
648	
649	// --- Settings ---
650	
651	export function getSetting<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
652	  const row = getDb().prepare("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?").get(key) as
653	    | { value: string }
654	    | undefined;
655	  if (!row) return fallback;
656	  try {
657	    return JSON.parse(row.value) as T;
658	  } catch {
659	    return fallback;
660	  }
661	}
662	
663	export function setSetting<T>(key: string, value: T): void {
664	  getDb()
665	    .prepare("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO settings (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)")
666	    .run(key, JSON.stringify(value));
667	}
668	
669	// --- Cleanup ---
670	
671	export function closeDb(): void {
672	  if (db) {
673	    db.close();
674	    db = null;
675	  }
676	}
677	```

685	import Database from "better-sqlite3";

691	// We test the db module by overriding the db path to a temp file.
692	// Since getDb() is a singleton, we test the query functions directly
693	// by creating a temp database and running migrations manually.
694	
695	function createTestDb(): Database.Database {
696	  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "clui-test-"));
697	  const dbPath = path.join(tmpDir, "test.db");
698	  const db = new Database(dbPath);

702	  // Run migration manually

708	  db.exec(sql);
709	
710	  return db;

742	    ).run(id, "test session", "active", now, now);

802	### Task 5: IPC Protocol
803	
804	**Files:**

808	- [ ] **Step 1: Write IPC types and helpers**
809	
810	```typescript
811	// src/shared/ipc.ts
812	
813	import net from "node:net";
814	import path from "node:path";
815	import { config } from "./config.js";
816	import type { Pin, StatusBarData, CompactEvent } from "./types.js";
817	
818	// --- Message Types ---
819	
820	// Shell → Worker
821	export type ShellToWorkerMessage =
822	  | { type: "send_prompt"; prompt: string; pins: Pin[] }
823	  | { type: "context_reset" }
824	  | { type: "context_snapshot" }
825	  | { type: "get_context_summary" }
826	  | {
827	      type: "set_worktree";
828	      worktreePath: string | null;
829	      branch: string | null;
830	    }
831	  | { type: "shutdown" };
832	
833	// Worker → Shell
834	export type WorkerToShellMessage =
835	  | { type: "ready"; workerId: string }
836	  | { type: "stream_chunk"; content: string }
837	  | { type: "message_complete"; role: string; content: string; tokens: number; cost: number }
838	  | { type: "context_compacted"; event: CompactEvent }
839	  | { type: "context_summary"; summary: string }
840	  | { type: "status_update"; data: Partial<StatusBarData> }
841	  | { type: "error"; message: string; fatal: boolean }
842	  | { type: "snapshot_complete"; filePath: string; summary: string };
843	
844	export type IPCMessage = ShellToWorkerMessage | WorkerToShellMessage;
845	
846	// --- Framing ---
847	// Length-prefixed JSON: 4-byte big-endian length + JSON payload
848	
849	const HEADER_SIZE = 4;
850	
851	export function encodeMessage(msg: IPCMessage): Buffer {
852	  const json = JSON.stringify(msg);
853	  const payload = Buffer.from(json, "utf-8");
854	  const header = Buffer.alloc(HEADER_SIZE);
855	  header.writeUInt32BE(payload.length, 0);
856	  return Buffer.concat([header, payload]);
857	}
858	
859	export class MessageDecoder {
860	  private buffer = Buffer.alloc(0);
861	
862	  feed(data: Buffer): IPCMessage[] {
863	    this.buffer = Buffer.concat([this.buffer, data]);
864	    const messages: IPCMessage[] = [];
865	
866	    while (this.buffer.length >= HEADER_SIZE) {
867	      const length = this.buffer.readUInt32BE(0);
868	      if (this.buffer.length < HEADER_SIZE + length) break;
869	
870	      const json = this.buffer.subarray(HEADER_SIZE, HEADER_SIZE + length).toString("utf-8");
871	      this.buffer = this.buffer.subarray(HEADER_SIZE + length);
872	
873	      try {
874	        messages.push(JSON.parse(json));
875	      } catch {
876	        // Skip malformed messages
877	      }
878	    }
879	
880	    return messages;
881	  }
882	}
883	
884	// --- Socket Helpers ---
885	
886	export function socketPath(workerId: string): string {
887	  return path.join(config.socketDir, `worker-${workerId}.sock`);
888	}
889	
890	export function sendMessage(socket: net.Socket, msg: IPCMessage): void {
891	  socket.write(encodeMessage(msg));
892	}
893	
894	export function createIPCServer(
895	  sockPath: string,
896	  onMessage: (msg: IPCMessage, socket: net.Socket) => void
897	): net.Server {
898	  const server = net.createServer((socket) => {
899	    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();
900	    socket.on("data", (data) => {
901	      for (const msg of decoder.feed(data)) {
902	        onMessage(msg, socket);
903	      }
904	    });
905	  });
906	
907	  server.listen(sockPath);
908	  return server;
909	}
910	
911	export function connectIPC(
912	  sockPath: string,
913	  onMessage: (msg: IPCMessage) => void
914	): Promise<net.Socket> {
915	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
916	    const socket = net.createConnection(sockPath, () => {
917	      const decoder = new MessageDecoder();
918	      socket.on("data", (data) => {
919	        for (const msg of decoder.feed(data)) {
920	          onMessage(msg);
921	        }
922	      });
923	      resolve(socket);
924	    });
925	    socket.on("error", reject);
926	  });
927	}
928	```
929

932	```typescript
933	// test/shared/ipc.test.ts
934	
935	import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
936	import { encodeMessage, MessageDecoder } from "../../src/shared/ipc.js";
937	import type { IPCMessage } from "../../src/shared/ipc.js";
938	
939	describe("IPC framing", () => {
940	  it("encodes and decodes a single message", () => {
941	    const msg: IPCMessage = { type: "ready", workerId: "w1" };
942	    const encoded = encodeMessage(msg);
943	    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();
944	    const decoded = decoder.feed(encoded);
945	    expect(decoded).toEqual([msg]);
946	  });
947	
948	  it("handles multiple messages in one buffer", () => {
949	    const msg1: IPCMessage = { type: "ready", workerId: "w1" };
950	    const msg2: IPCMessage = { type: "stream_chunk", content: "hello" };
951	    const buf = Buffer.concat([encodeMessage(msg1), encodeMessage(msg2)]);
952	    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();
953	    const decoded = decoder.feed(buf);
954	    expect(decoded).toEqual([msg1, msg2]);
955	  });
956	
957	  it("handles partial messages across feeds", () => {
958	    const msg: IPCMessage = { type: "stream_chunk", content: "world" };
959	    const full = encodeMessage(msg);
960	    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();
961	
962	    // Feed first half
963	    const mid = Math.floor(full.length / 2);
964	    const part1 = decoder.feed(full.subarray(0, mid));
965	    expect(part1).toEqual([]);
966	
967	    // Feed second half
968	    const part2 = decoder.feed(full.subarray(mid));
969	    expect(part2).toEqual([msg]);
970	  });
971	});
972	```

999	```typescript
1000	// src/worker/claude-bridge.ts
1001	
1002	import { query, type SDKMessage } from "@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk";
1003	import type { Pin, CompactEvent, StatusBarData } from "../shared/types.js";
1004	
1005	export interface BridgeCallbacks {
1006	  onStreamChunk: (content: string) => void;
1007	  onMessageComplete: (role: string, content: string, tokens: number, cost: number) => void;
1008	  onCompacted: (event: CompactEvent) => void;
1009	  onStatusUpdate: (data: Partial<StatusBarData>) => void;
1010	  onError: (message: string, fatal: boolean) => void;
1011	}
1012	
1013	export interface BridgeOptions {
1014	  cwd?: string;
1015	  model?: string;
1016	  sessionId?: string;
1017	  resume?: string;
1018	}
1019	
1020	export class ClaudeBridge {
1021	  private currentQuery: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof query>> | null = null;
1022	  private callbacks: BridgeCallbacks;
1023	  private options: BridgeOptions;
1024	  private claudeSessionId: string | null = null;
1025	
1026	  constructor(callbacks: BridgeCallbacks, options: BridgeOptions = {}) {
1027	    this.callbacks = callbacks;
1028	    this.options = options;
1029	  }
1030	
1031	  async sendPrompt(prompt: string, pins: Pin[]): Promise<void> {
1032	    const systemPromptParts: string[] = [];
1033	
1034	    // Resolve pins into context
1035	    for (const pin of pins) {
1036	      if (pin.type === "file") {
1037	        systemPromptParts.push(`[Pinned file: ${pin.target}]`);
1038	      } else if (pin.type === "skill") {
1039	        systemPromptParts.push(`[Active skill: ${pin.target}]`);
1040	      } else if (pin.type === "command") {
1041	        systemPromptParts.push(`[Command: ${pin.target}]`);
1042	      }
1043	    }
1044	
1045	    const fullPrompt = systemPromptParts.length > 0
1046	      ? `${systemPromptParts.join("\n")}\n\n${prompt}`
1047	      : prompt;
1048	
1049	    try {
1050	      const queryOptions: Record<string, any> = {
1051	        allowedTools: ["Read", "Edit", "Write", "Bash", "Glob", "Grep"],
1052	        includePartialMessages: true,
1053	      };
1054	
1055	      if (this.options.cwd) queryOptions.cwd = this.options.cwd;
1056	      if (this.options.model) queryOptions.model = this.options.model;
1057	
1058	      // Resume existing session or start new
1059	      if (this.claudeSessionId) {
1060	        queryOptions.resume = this.claudeSessionId;
1061	      } else if (this.options.resume) {
1062	        queryOptions.resume = this.options.resume;
1063	      }
1064	
1065	      this.currentQuery = query({ prompt: fullPrompt, options: queryOptions });
1066	
1067	      for await (const msg of this.currentQuery) {
1068	        this.handleMessage(msg);
1069	      }
1070	    } catch (err: any) {
1071	      this.callbacks.onError(err.message || "Unknown error", false);
1072	    } finally {
1073	      this.currentQuery = null;
1074	    }
1075	  }
1076	
1077	  private handleMessage(msg: SDKMessage): void {
1078	    // Type narrowing based on message structure
1079	    if ("type" in msg) {
1080	      switch (msg.type) {
1081	        case "system":
1082	          if ("subtype" in msg && msg.subtype === "init") {
1083	            this.claudeSessionId = (msg as any).session_id ?? null;
1084	            this.callbacks.onStatusUpdate({
1085	              model: (msg as any).model ?? null,
1086	              sessionId: this.claudeSessionId,
1087	            });
1088	          }
1089	          if ("subtype" in msg && msg.subtype === "compact_boundary") {
1090	            const meta = (msg as any).compact_metadata;
1091	            this.callbacks.onCompacted({
1092	              timestamp: Date.now(),
1093	              trigger: meta?.trigger ?? "auto",
1094	              preTokens: meta?.pre_tokens ?? 0,
1095	            });
1096	          }
1097	          break;
1098	
1099	        case "assistant":
1100	          // Full assistant message
1101	          const assistantMsg = msg as any;
1102	          const content = assistantMsg.message?.content
1103	            ?.filter((b: any) => b.type === "text")
1104	            .map((b: any) => b.text)
1105	            .join("") ?? "";
1106	          const usage = assistantMsg.message?.usage;
1107	          const tokens = (usage?.input_tokens ?? 0) + (usage?.output_tokens ?? 0);
1108	          this.callbacks.onMessageComplete("assistant", content, tokens, 0);
1109	          break;
1110	
1111	        case "stream_event":
1112	          // Partial streaming
1113	          const event = (msg as any).event;
1114	          if (event?.type === "content_block_delta" && event.delta?.type === "text_delta") {
1115	            this.callbacks.onStreamChunk(event.delta.text);
1116	          }
1117	          break;
1118	
1119	        case "result":
1120	          const result = msg as any;
1121	          this.callbacks.onStatusUpdate({
1122	            sessionId: result.session_id ?? this.claudeSessionId,
1123	          });
1124	          if (result.subtype === "error_during_execution") {
1125	            this.callbacks.onError(result.error ?? "Execution error", false);
1126	          }
1127	          break;
1128	      }
1129	    }
1130	  }
1131	
1132	  interrupt(): void {
1133	    this.currentQuery?.interrupt();
1134	  }
1135	
1136	  getSessionId(): string | null {
1137	    return this.claudeSessionId;
1138	  }
1139	}
1140	```

1249	// src/shell/store/tabs.ts
1250	
1251	import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
1252	import type { TabState, StatusBarData, CompactEvent } from "../../shared/types.js";
1253	import type { Message } from "../../shared/types.js";
1254	
1255	export interface TabsSlice {
1256	  tabs: TabState[];
1257	  activeTabId: string | null;
1258	  tabMessages: Record<string, Message[]>;
1259	  tabStreamBuffer: Record<string, string>;
1260	  tabStatus: Record<string, StatusBarData>;
1261	  tabCompactEvents: Record<string, CompactEvent[]>;
1262	
1263	  createTab: (name?: string) => TabState;
1264	  closeTab: (tabId: string) => void;
1265	  setActiveTab: (tabId: string) => void;
1266	  setWorkerReady: (tabId: string, workerId: string) => void;
1267	  appendMessage: (tabId: string, msg: Message) => void;
1268	  setStreamBuffer: (tabId: string, content: string) => void;
1269	  appendStreamBuffer: (tabId: string, chunk: string) => void;
1270	  clearStreamBuffer: (tabId: string) => void;
1271	  updateTabStatus: (tabId: string, data: Partial<StatusBarData>) => void;
1272	  addCompactEvent: (tabId: string, event: CompactEvent) => void;
1273	  renameTab: (tabId: string, name: string) => void;
1274	}
1275	
1276	export function createTabsSlice(
1277	  set: (fn: (state: any) => any) => void,
1278	  get: () => any
1279	): TabsSlice {
1280	  return {
1281	    tabs: [],
1282	    activeTabId: null,
1283	    tabMessages: {},
1284	    tabStreamBuffer: {},
1285	    tabStatus: {},
1286	    tabCompactEvents: {},
1287	
1288	    createTab: (name?: string) => {
1289	      const state = get();
1290	      const index = state.tabs.length + 1;
1291	      const tab: TabState = {
1292	        id: uuid(),
1293	        index,
1294	        sessionId: uuid(),
1295	        name: name || `Session ${index}`,
1296	        workerId: null,
1297	        workerReady: false,
1298	        worktreePath: null,
1299	        worktreeBranch: null,
1300	      };
1301	
1302	      set((s: any) => ({
1303	        tabs: [...s.tabs, tab],
1304	        activeTabId: tab.id,
1305	        tabMessages: { ...s.tabMessages, [tab.id]: [] },
1306	        tabStreamBuffer: { ...s.tabStreamBuffer, [tab.id]: "" },
1307	        tabStatus: { ...s.tabStatus, [tab.id]: createEmptyStatus() },
1308	        tabCompactEvents: { ...s.tabCompactEvents, [tab.id]: [] },
1309	      }));
1310	
1311	      return tab;
1312	    },
1313	
1314	    closeTab: (tabId: string) => {
1315	      set((s: any) => {
1316	        const remaining = s.tabs.filter((t: TabState) => t.id !== tabId);
1317	        const { [tabId]: _m, ...restMessages } = s.tabMessages;
1318	        const { [tabId]: _s, ...restStream } = s.tabStreamBuffer;
1319	        const { [tabId]: _st, ...restStatus } = s.tabStatus;
1320	        const { [tabId]: _c, ...restCompact } = s.tabCompactEvents;
1321	
1322	        return {
1323	          tabs: remaining,
1324	          activeTabId:
1325	            s.activeTabId === tabId
1326	              ? remaining[remaining.length - 1]?.id ?? null
1327	              : s.activeTabId,
1328	          tabMessages: restMessages,
1329	          tabStreamBuffer: restStream,
1330	          tabStatus: restStatus,
1331	          tabCompactEvents: restCompact,
1332	        };
1333	      });
1334	    },
1335	
1336	    setActiveTab: (tabId: string) => set(() => ({ activeTabId: tabId })),
1337	
1338	    setWorkerReady: (tabId: string, workerId: string) => {
1339	      set((s: any) => ({
1340	        tabs: s.tabs.map((t: TabState) =>
1341	          t.id === tabId ? { ...t, workerId, workerReady: true } : t
1342	        ),
1343	      }));
1344	    },
1345	
1346	    appendMessage: (tabId: string, msg: Message) => {
1347	      set((s: any) => ({
1348	        tabMessages: {
1349	          ...s.tabMessages,
1350	          [tabId]: [...(s.tabMessages[tabId] || []), msg],
1351	        },
1352	      }));
1353	    },
1354	
1355	    setStreamBuffer: (tabId: string, content: string) => {
1356	      set((s: any) => ({
1357	        tabStreamBuffer: { ...s.tabStreamBuffer, [tabId]: content },
1358	      }));
1359	    },
1360	
1361	    appendStreamBuffer: (tabId: string, chunk: string) => {
1362	      set((s: any) => ({
1363	        tabStreamBuffer: {
1364	          ...s.tabStreamBuffer,
1365	          [tabId]: (s.tabStreamBuffer[tabId] || "") + chunk,
1366	        },
1367	      }));
1368	    },
1369	
1370	    clearStreamBuffer: (tabId: string) => {
1371	      set((s: any) => ({
1372	        tabStreamBuffer: { ...s.tabStreamBuffer, [tabId]: "" },
1373	      }));
1374	    },
1375	
1376	    updateTabStatus: (tabId: string, data: Partial<StatusBarData>) => {
1377	      set((s: any) => ({
1378	        tabStatus: {
1379	          ...s.tabStatus,
1380	          [tabId]: { ...(s.tabStatus[tabId] || createEmptyStatus()), ...data },
1381	        },
1382	      }));
1383	    },
1384	
1385	    addCompactEvent: (tabId: string, event: CompactEvent) => {
1386	      set((s: any) => ({
1387	        tabCompactEvents: {
1388	          ...s.tabCompactEvents,
1389	          [tabId]: [...(s.tabCompactEvents[tabId] || []), event],
1390	        },
1391	      }));
1392	    },
1393	
1394	    renameTab: (tabId: string, name: string) => {
1395	      set((s: any) => ({
1396	        tabs: s.tabs.map((t: TabState) =>
1397	          t.id === tabId ? { ...t, name } : t
1398	        ),
1399	      }));
1400	    },
1401	  };
1402	}
1403	
1404	function createEmptyStatus(): StatusBarData {
1405	  return {
1406	    model: null,
1407	    totalTokens: 0,
1408	    contextTokens: 0,
1409	    contextMaxTokens: 1000000,
1410	    contextPercent: 0,
1411	    style: null,
1412	    sessionId: null,
1413	    durationSeconds: 0,
1414	    cost: 0,
1415	    gitBranch: null,
1416	    gitDiffStats: null,
1417	    compactedAt: null,
1418	  };
1419	}
1420	```

1600	### Task 9: TabBar Component
1601	
1602	**Files:**
1603	- Create: `src/shell/components/TabBar.tsx`
1604	
1605	- [ ] **Step 1: Write tab bar**
1606	
1607	```tsx
1608	// src/shell/components/TabBar.tsx
1609	
1610	import React from "react";
1611	import { Box, Text, useInput } from "ink";
1612	import { useStore } from "../store/index.js";
1613	
1614	export function TabBar(): React.ReactElement {
1615	  const tabs = useStore((s) => s.tabs);
1616	  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);
1617	  const setActiveTab = useStore((s) => s.setActiveTab);
1618	  const createTab = useStore((s) => s.createTab);
1619	  const closeTab = useStore((s) => s.closeTab);
1620	
1621	  useInput((input, key) => {
1622	    // Ctrl+T — new tab
1623	    if (input === "t" && key.ctrl) {
1624	      createTab();
1625	      return;
1626	    }
1627	
1628	    // Ctrl+W — close active tab
1629	    if (input === "w" && key.ctrl && activeTabId) {
1630	      closeTab(activeTabId);
1631	      return;
1632	    }
1633	
1634	    // Ctrl+1..9 — switch to tab by index
1635	    if (key.ctrl && input >= "1" && input <= "9") {
1636	      const idx = parseInt(input, 10) - 1;
1637	      if (tabs[idx]) {
1638	        setActiveTab(tabs[idx].id);
1639	      }
1640	      return;
1641	    }
1642	
1643	    // Tab key — cycle tabs
1644	    if (key.tab && !key.shift && tabs.length > 1) {
1645	      const currentIdx = tabs.findIndex((t) => t.id === activeTabId);
1646	      const nextIdx = (currentIdx + 1) % tabs.length;
1647	      setActiveTab(tabs[nextIdx].id);
1648	    }
1649	  });
1650	
1651	  return (
1652	    <Box flexDirection="row" borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray" borderBottom={false}>
1653	      {tabs.map((tab) => (
1654	        <Box key={tab.id} paddingX={1}>
1655	          <Text
1656	            color={tab.id === activeTabId ? "cyan" : "gray"}
1657	            bold={tab.id === activeTabId}
1658	          >
1659	            {tab.index}: {tab.name}
1660	          </Text>
1661	        </Box>
1662	      ))}
1663	      <Box paddingX={1}>
1664	        <Text color="green">[+]</Text>
1665	      </Box>
1666	    </Box>
1667	  );
1668	}
1669	```

1758	// src/shell/components/InputBar.tsx
1759	
1760	import React, { useState } from "react";
1761	import { Box, Text } from "ink";
1762	import TextInput from "ink-text-input";
1763	
1764	interface InputBarProps {
1765	  onSubmit: (text: string) => void;
1766	  disabled?: boolean;
1767	}
1768	
1769	export function InputBar({ onSubmit, disabled = false }: InputBarProps): React.ReactElement {
1770	  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
1771	
1772	  function handleSubmit(text: string): void {
1773	    const trimmed = text.trim();
1774	    if (!trimmed) return;
1775	    onSubmit(trimmed);
1776	    setValue("");
1777	  }
1778	
1779	  return (
1780	    <Box borderStyle="single" borderColor="gray" borderTop={false} paddingX={1}>
1781	      <Text color="green">❯ </Text>
1782	      <TextInput
1783	        value={value}
1784	        onChange={setValue}
1785	        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
1786	        placeholder={disabled ? "Waiting for response..." : "Type a message..."}
1787	        focus={!disabled}
1788	      />
1789	    </Box>
1790	  );
1791	}
1792	```

1803	### Task 12: StatusBar Component
1804	
1805	**Files:**
1806	- Create: `src/shell/components/StatusBar.tsx`
1807	
1808	- [ ] **Step 1: Write configurable status bar**
1809	
1810	```tsx
1811	// src/shell/components/StatusBar.tsx
1812	
1813	import React from "react";
1814	import { Box, Text } from "ink";
1815	import { useStore } from "../store/index.js";
1816	import type { StatusBarData, StatusBarField } from "../../shared/types.js";
1817	import { config } from "../../shared/config.js";
1818	
1819	function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
1820	  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;
1821	  const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
1822	  const s = seconds % 60;
1823	  return s > 0 ? `${m}m${s}s` : `${m}m`;
1824	}
1825	
1826	function formatCost(cost: number): string {
1827	  return `$${cost.toFixed(2)}`;
1828	}
1829	
1830	function formatTokens(n: number): string {
1831	  if (n >= 1000000) return `${(n / 1000000).toFixed(1)}M`;
1832	  if (n >= 1000) return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(1)}k`;
1833	  return `${n}`;
1834	}
1835	
1836	function renderField(
1837	  key: string,
1838	  label: string,
1839	  data: StatusBarData
1840	): React.ReactElement | null {
1841	  switch (key) {
1842	    case "model":
1843	      return data.model ? (
1844	        <Text color="green">{data.model}</Text>
1845	      ) : null;
1846	    case "totalTokens":
1847	      return (
1848	        <Text color="yellow">
1849	          Total: {formatTokens(data.totalTokens)}
1850	        </Text>
1851	      );
1852	    case "contextTokens":
1853	      return (
1854	        <Text color="yellow">
1855	          Ctx: {formatTokens(data.contextTokens)}
1856	        </Text>
1857	      );
1858	    case "contextPercent": {
1859	      const pct = data.contextPercent;
1860	      const color = pct > 80 ? "red" : pct > 50 ? "yellow" : "green";
1861	      return (
1862	        <Text color={color}>
1863	          Ctx: {pct.toFixed(1)}%
1864	        </Text>
1865	      );
1866	    }
1867	    case "cost":
1868	      return <Text color="cyan">{formatCost(data.cost)}</Text>;
1869	    case "durationSeconds":
1870	      return (
1871	        <Text color="magenta">
1872	          {formatDuration(data.durationSeconds)}
1873	        </Text>
1874	      );
1875	    case "gitBranch":
1876	      return data.gitBranch ? (
1877	        <Text color="yellow">⎇ {data.gitBranch}</Text>
1878	      ) : (
1879	        <Text color="gray">⎇ no git</Text>
1880	      );
1881	    case "gitDiffStats":
1882	      if (!data.gitDiffStats) return null;
1883	      const { added, removed, modified } = data.gitDiffStats;
1884	      return (
1885	        <Text>
1886	          <Text color="green">+{added}</Text>
1887	          <Text> </Text>
1888	          <Text color="red">-{removed}</Text>
1889	          <Text> </Text>
1890	          <Text color="yellow">~{modified}</Text>
1891	        </Text>
1892	      );
1893	    case "style":
1894	      return data.style ? (
1895	        <Text color="white">Style: {data.style}</Text>
1896	      ) : null;
1897	    case "sessionId":
1898	      return data.sessionId ? (
1899	        <Text color="gray">{data.sessionId.slice(0, 8)}</Text>
1900	      ) : null;
1901	    default:
1902	      return null;
1903	  }
1904	}
1905	
1906	interface StatusBarProps {
1907	  fields?: StatusBarField[];
1908	}
1909	
1910	export function StatusBar({ fields }: StatusBarProps): React.ReactElement {
1911	  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);
1912	  const data = useStore((s) =>
1913	    activeTabId ? s.tabStatus[activeTabId] : null
1914	  );
1915	  const compactEvents = useStore((s) =>
1916	    activeTabId ? s.tabCompactEvents[activeTabId] || [] : []
1917	  );
1918	
1919	  const enabledFields = (fields || config.defaultStatusBarFields).filter(
1920	    (f) => f.enabled
1921	  );
1922	
1923	  if (!data) {
1924	    return (
1925	      <Box paddingX={1}>
1926	        <Text color="gray">No active session</Text>
1927	      </Box>
1928	    );
1929	  }
1930	
1931	  const lastCompact = compactEvents[compactEvents.length - 1];
1932	
1933	  return (
1934	    <Box paddingX={1} flexDirection="row" gap={1}>
1935	      {enabledFields.map((field) => {
1936	        const rendered = renderField(field.key, field.label, data);
1937	        if (!rendered) return null;
1938	        return (
1939	          <React.Fragment key={field.key}>
1940	            {rendered}
1941	            <Text color="gray"> │ </Text>
1942	          </React.Fragment>
1943	        );
1944	      })}
1945	      {lastCompact && (
1946	        <Text color="red">⚡ compacted</Text>
1947	      )}
1948	    </Box>
1949	  );
1950	}
1951	```

1978	  onRemovePin: (pinId: string) => void;

1981	export function ContextBar({ pins, onRemovePin }: ContextBarProps): React.ReactElement {

1983

1993	          </Text>
1994	        </Box>
1995	      ))}
1996	      {oneshotPins.map((pin) => (
1997	        <Box key={pin.id} marginRight={1}>
1998	          <Text color="yellow">
1999	            1️⃣ {pin.target}
2000	          </Text>
2001	        </Box>
2002	      ))}
2003	    </Box>
2004	  );
2005	}

2017	### Task 14: App Entry Point
2018	
2019	**Files:**

2021	- Create: `src/index.tsx`
2022	- Create: `src/cli/index.ts`
2023	
2024	- [ ] **Step 1: Write CLI arg parser**
2025	
2026	```typescript
2027	// src/cli/index.ts
2028	
2029	export interface CliArgs {
2030	  cwd: string;
2031	  session?: string;
2032	  model?: string;
2033	}
2034	
2035	export function parseArgs(argv: string[]): CliArgs {
2036	  const args: CliArgs = { cwd: process.cwd() };
2037	
2038	  for (let i = 2; i < argv.length; i++) {
2039	    const arg = argv[i];
2040	    switch (arg) {
2041	      case "--cwd":
2042	        args.cwd = argv[++i] || process.cwd();
2043	        break;
2044	      case "--session":
2045	        args.session = argv[++i];
2046	        break;
2047	      case "--model":
2048	        args.model = argv[++i];
2049	        break;

2200	  server: net.Server;
2201	  workerId: string;
2202	  tabId: string;
2203	}
2204	
2205	export function useWorkerManager(cwd: string) {
2206	  const workersRef = useRef<Map<string, WorkerHandle>>(new Map());
2207	
2208	  const spawnWorker = useCallback(
2209	    (tabId: string): string => {
2210	      ensureDirs();
2211	      const workerId = uuid();
2212	      const sockPath = socketPath(workerId);
2213	
2214	      // Clean up stale socket
2215	      try {
2216	        fs.unlinkSync(sockPath);
2217	      } catch {}
2218	
2219	      // Create IPC server for this worker
2220	      const server = createIPCServer(sockPath, (msg, socket) => {
2221	        const handle = workersRef.current.get(tabId);
2222	        if (handle && !handle.socket) {
2223	          handle.socket = socket;
2224	        }
2225	        handleWorkerMessage(tabId, msg as WorkerToShellMessage);
2226	      });
2227	
2228	      // Fork worker process
2229	      const workerProcess = fork(config.workerEntry, [workerId, cwd], {
2230	        stdio: "pipe",
2231	        env: { ...process.env },
2232	      });
2233	
2234	      workerProcess.on("exit", (code) => {
2235	        cleanupWorker(tabId);
2236	        if (code !== 0) {
2237	          useStore.getState().appendMessage(tabId, {
2238	            id: uuid(),
2239	            sessionId: tabId,
2240	            role: "system",
2241	            content: `Worker exited with code ${code}`,
2242	            tokens: 0,
2243	            cost: 0,
2244	            createdAt: Date.now(),
2245	          });
2246	        }
2247	      });
2248	
2249	      workersRef.current.set(tabId, {
2250	        process: workerProcess,
2251	        socket: null,
2252	        server,
2253	        workerId,
2254	        tabId,
2255	      });
2256	
2257	      return workerId;
2258	    },
2259	    [cwd]
2260	  );
2261	
2262	  function handleWorkerMessage(tabId: string, msg: WorkerToShellMessage): void {
2263	    const store = useStore.getState();
2264	
2265	    switch (msg.type) {
2266	      case "ready":
2267	        store.setWorkerReady(tabId, msg.workerId);
2268	        break;
2269	
2270	      case "stream_chunk":
2271	        store.appendStreamBuffer(tabId, msg.content);
2272	        break;
2273	
2274	      case "message_complete":
2275	        store.clearStreamBuffer(tabId);
2276	        store.appendMessage(tabId, {
2277	          id: uuid(),
2278	          sessionId: tabId,
2279	          role: msg.role as any,
2280	          content: msg.content,
2281	          tokens: msg.tokens,
2282	          cost: msg.cost,
2283	          createdAt: Date.now(),
2284	        });
2285	        break;
2286	
2287	      case "status_update":
2288	        store.updateTabStatus(tabId, msg.data);
2289	        break;
2290	
2291	      case "context_compacted":
2292	        store.addCompactEvent(tabId, msg.event);
2293	        break;
2294	
2295	      case "error":
2296	        store.appendMessage(tabId, {
2297	          id: uuid(),
2298	          sessionId: tabId,
2299	          role: "system",
2300	          content: `Error: ${msg.message}`,
2301	          tokens: 0,
2302	          cost: 0,
2303	          createdAt: Date.now(),
2304	        });
2305	        break;
2306	    }
2307	  }
2308	
2309	  function sendToWorker(tabId: string, msg: ShellToWorkerMessage): void {
2310	    const handle = workersRef.current.get(tabId);
2311	    if (handle?.socket) {
2312	      sendMessage(handle.socket, msg);
2313	    }
2314	  }
2315	
2316	  function killWorker(tabId: string): void {
2317	    const handle = workersRef.current.get(tabId);
2318	    if (handle) {
2319	      sendToWorker(tabId, { type: "shutdown" });
2320	      setTimeout(() => {
2321	        handle.process.kill("SIGTERM");
2322	        cleanupWorker(tabId);
2323	      }, 2000);
2324	    }
2325	  }
2326	
2327	  function cleanupWorker(tabId: string): void {
2328	    const handle = workersRef.current.get(tabId);
2329	    if (handle) {
2330	      handle.socket?.destroy();
2331	      handle.server.close();
2332	      try {
2333	        fs.unlinkSync(socketPath(handle.workerId));
2334	      } catch {}
2335	      workersRef.current.delete(tabId);
2336	    }
2337	  }
2338	
2339	  // Cleanup all workers on unmount
2340	  useEffect(() => {
2341	    return () => {
2342	      for (const [tabId] of workersRef.current) {
2343	        killWorker(tabId);
2344	      }
2345	    };
2346	  }, []);
2347	
2348	  return {
2349	    spawnWorker,
2350	    sendToWorker,
2351	    killWorker,
2352	    sendPrompt: (tabId: string, prompt: string, pins: Pin[]) => {
2353	      const store = useStore.getState();
2354	      // Add user message to store
2355	      store.appendMessage(tabId, {
2356	        id: uuid(),
2357	        sessionId: tabId,
2358	        role: "user",
2359	        content: prompt,
2360	        tokens: 0,
2361	        cost: 0,
2362	        createdAt: Date.now(),
2363	      });
2364	      // Send to worker
2365	      sendToWorker(tabId, { type: "send_prompt", prompt, pins });
2366	    },
2367	  };
2368	}

2499	```typescript
2500	// src/shell/store/sessions.ts
2501	
2502	import type { Session } from "../../shared/types.js";
2503	
2504	export interface SessionsSlice {
2505	  sessions: Session[];
2506	  sessionFilter: string;
2507	  setSessions: (sessions: Session[]) => void;
2508	  setSessionFilter: (filter: string) => void;
2509	  updateSessionColor: (sessionId: string, color: string) => void;
2510	  updateSessionName: (sessionId: string, name: string) => void;
2511	  filteredSessions: () => Session[];
2512	}
2513	
2514	export function createSessionsSlice(
2515	  set: (fn: (state: any) => any) => void,
2516	  get: () => any
2517	): SessionsSlice {
2518	  return {
2519	    sessions: [],
2520	    sessionFilter: "",
2521	
2522	    setSessions: (sessions: Session[]) => set(() => ({ sessions })),
2523	
2524	    setSessionFilter: (filter: string) => set(() => ({ sessionFilter: filter })),
2525	
2526	    updateSessionColor: (sessionId: string, color: string) => {
2527	      set((s: any) => ({
2528	        sessions: s.sessions.map((session: Session) =>
2529	          session.id === sessionId ? { ...session, color } : session
2530	        ),
2531	      }));
2532	    },
2533	
2534	    updateSessionName: (sessionId: string, name: string) => {
2535	      set((s: any) => ({
2536	        sessions: s.sessions.map((session: Session) =>
2537	          session.id === sessionId ? { ...session, name } : session
2538	        ),
2539	      }));
2540	    },
2541	
2542	    filteredSessions: () => {
2543	      const state = get();
2544	      const filter = state.sessionFilter.toLowerCase();
2545	      if (!filter) return state.sessions;
2546	      return state.sessions.filter((s: Session) =>
2547	        s.name.toLowerCase().includes(filter)
2548	      );
2549	    },
2550	  };
2551	}
2552	```

2580	### Task 18: useSessions Hook
2581	
2582	**Files:**
2583	- Create: `src/shell/hooks/useSessions.ts`
2584	
2585	- [ ] **Step 1: Write hook to load sessions from Claude Agent SDK**
2586	
2587	```typescript
2588	// src/shell/hooks/useSessions.ts
2589	
2590	import { useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
2591	import { useStore } from "../store/index.js";
2592	import type { Session } from "../../shared/types.js";
2593	import * as db from "../../shared/db.js";
2594	
2595	// Dynamic import to handle case where SDK is not available
2596	async function loadClaudeSessions(): Promise<Session[]> {
2597	  try {
2598	    const sdk = await import("@anthropic-ai/claude-agent-sdk");
2599	    const sdkSessions = await sdk.listSessions({ limit: 100 });
2600	
2601	    return sdkSessions.map((s: any) => ({
2602	      id: s.sessionId || s.id,
2603	      claudeSessionId: s.sessionId || s.id,
2604	      name: s.title || s.name || `Session ${s.sessionId?.slice(0, 8)}`,
2605	      color: null,
2606	      repoPath: s.cwd || null,
2607	      worktreePath: null,
2608	      worktreeBranch: null,
2609	      model: s.model || null,
2610	      totalTokens: 0,
2611	      totalCost: 0,
2612	      durationSeconds: 0,
2613	      status: "closed" as const,
2614	      createdAt: s.createdAt || Date.now(),
2615	      updatedAt: s.updatedAt || Date.now(),
2616	    }));
2617	  } catch {
2618	    return [];
2619	  }
2620	}
2621	
2622	export function useSessions(): {
2623	  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
2624	} {
2625	  const setSessions = useStore((s) => s.setSessions);
2626	
2627	  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
2628	    // Merge: Claude SDK sessions + local DB sessions
2629	    const [sdkSessions, dbSessions] = await Promise.all([
2630	      loadClaudeSessions(),
2631	      Promise.resolve(db.listSessions(100)),
2632	    ]);
2633	
2634	    // DB sessions override SDK sessions (for color, name overrides)
2635	    const dbMap = new Map(dbSessions.map((s) => [s.claudeSessionId || s.id, s]));
2636	
2637	    const merged: Session[] = [];
2638	    const seen = new Set<string>();
2639	
2640	    // SDK sessions first, enriched with DB overrides
2641	    for (const sdkSession of sdkSessions) {
2642	      const dbOverride = dbMap.get(sdkSession.id);
2643	      if (dbOverride) {
2644	        merged.push({
2645	          ...sdkSession,
2646	          name: dbOverride.name || sdkSession.name,
2647	          color: dbOverride.color || sdkSession.color,
2648	        });
2649	      } else {
2650	        merged.push(sdkSession);
2651	      }
2652	      seen.add(sdkSession.id);
2653	    }
2654	
2655	    // DB-only sessions (created in clui, not from SDK)
2656	    for (const dbSession of dbSessions) {
2657	      if (!seen.has(dbSession.claudeSessionId || dbSession.id)) {
2658	        merged.push(dbSession);
2659	      }
2660	    }
2661	
2662	    // Sort by most recent
2663	    merged.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);
2664	    setSessions(merged);
2665	  }, [setSessions]);
2666	
2667	  useEffect(() => {
2668	    refresh();
2669	  }, [refresh]);
2670	
2671	  return { refresh };
2672	}
2673	```

The plan has 6 phases. I'll start with Phase 1 (Foundation) using parallel agents for independent tasks.

Phase 1 tasks:
1. Project scaffold (package.json, tsconfig, tsup, .gitignore)
2. Shared types
3. Config module
4. SQLite database layer
5. IPC protocol
6. Worker process / Claude bridge
7. Zustand store
8. UI components (Layout, TabBar, ChatView, InputBar, StatusBar, ContextBar)
9. App entry point

Tasks 1-5 are foundational and mostly independent. Let me start by scaffolding the project, then parallelize the rest.

\"error\"; message: string; fatal: boolean }\n  | { type: \"snapshot_complete\"; filePath: string; summary: string };\n\nexport type IPCMessage = ShellToWorkerMessage | WorkerToShellMessage;\n\nconst HEADER_SIZE = 4;\n\nexport function encodeMessage(msg: IPCMessage): Buffer {\n  const json = JSON.stringify(msg);\n  const payload = Buffer.from(json, \"utf-8\");\n  const header = Buffer.alloc(HEADER_SIZE);\n  header.writeUInt32BE(payload.length, 0);\n  return Buffer.concat([header, payload]);\n}\n\nexport class MessageDecoder {\n  private buffer = Buffer.alloc(0);\n\n  feed(data: Buffer): IPCMessage[] {\n    this.buffer = Buffer.concat([this.buffer, data]);\n    const messages: IPCMessage[] = [];\n\n    while (this.buffer.length >= HEADER_SIZE) {\n      const length = this.buffer.readUInt32BE(0);\n      if (this.buffer.length < HEADER_SIZE + length) break;\n\n      const json = this.buffer.subarray(HEADER_SIZE, HEADER_SIZE + length).toString(\"utf-8\");\n      this.buffer = this.buffer.subarray(HEADER_SIZE + length);\n\n      try {\n        messages.push(JSON.parse(json));\n      } catch {\n        // Skip malformed messages\n      }\n    }\n\n    return messages;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function socketPath(workerId: string): string {\n  return path.join(config.socketDir, `worker-${workerId}.sock`);\n}\n\nexport function sendMessage(socket: net.Socket, msg: IPCMessage): void {\n  socket.write(encodeMessage(msg));\n}\n\nexport function createIPCServer(\n  sockPath: string,\n  onMessage: (msg: IPCMessage, socket: net.Socket) => void\n): net.Server {\n  const server = net.createServer((socket) => {\n    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();\n    socket.on(\"data\", (data) => {\n      for (const msg of decoder.feed(data)) {\n        onMessage(msg, socket);\n      }\n    });\n  });\n\n  server.listen(sockPath);\n  return server;\n}\n\nexport function connectIPC(\n  sockPath: string,\n  onMessage: (msg: IPCMessage) => void\n): Promise<net.Socket> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const socket = net.createConnection(sockPath, () => {\n      const decoder = new MessageDecoder();\n      socket.on(\"data\", (data) => {\n total_cost, duration_seconds, status, created_at, updated_at)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`\n    )\n    .run(\n      session.id,\n      session.claudeSessionId,\n      session.name,\n      session.color,\n      session.repoPath,\n      session.worktreePath,\n      session.worktreeBranch,\n      session.model,\n      session.totalTokens,\n      session.totalCost,\n      session.durationSeconds,\n      session.status,\n      session.createdAt,\n      session.updatedAt\n    );\n}\n\nexport function updateSession(\n  id: string,\n  updates: Partial<\n    Pick<Session, \"name\" | \"color\" | \"status\" | \"totalTokens\" | \"totalCost\" | \"durationSeconds\" | \"claudeSessionId\" | \"worktreePath\" | \"worktreeBranch\" | \"model\">\n  >\n): void {\n  const fields: string[] = [];\n  const values: any[] = [];\n\n  const columnMap: Record<string, string> = {\n    name: \"name\",\n    color: \"color\",\n    status: \"status\",\n    totalTokens: \"total_tokens\",\n    totalCost: \"total_cost\",\n    durationSeconds: \"duration_seconds\",\n    claudeSessionId: \"claude_session_id\",\n    worktreePath: \"worktree_path\",\n    worktreeBranch: \"worktree_branch\",\n    model: \"model\",\n  };\n\n  for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(updates)) {\n    const col = columnMap[key];\n    if (col && val !== undefined) {\n      fields.push(`${col} = ?`);\n      values.push(val);\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (fields.length === 0) return;\n\n  fields.push(\"updated_at = ?\");\n  values.push(Date.now());\n  values.push(id);\n\n  getDb()\n    .prepare(`UPDATE sessions SET ${fields.join(\", \")} WHERE id = ?`)\n    .run(...values);\n}\n\nexport function listSessions(limit = 50): Session[] {\n  return getDb()\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT id, claude_session_id as claudeSessionId, name, color, repo_path as repoPath,\n              worktree_path as worktreePath, worktree_branch as worktreeBranch, model,\n              total_tokens as totalTokens, total_cost as totalCost,\n              duration_seconds as durationSeconds, status, created_at as createdAt,\n              updated_at as updatedAt\n       FROM sessions ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT ?`\n    )\n    .all(limit) as Session[];\n}\n\nexport function getSession(id: string): Session | undefined {\n  return getDb()\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT id, claude_session_id as claudeSessionId, name, color, repo_path as repoPath,\n              worktree_path as worktreePath, worktree_branch as worktreeBranch, model,\n              total_tokens as totalTokens, total_cost as totalCost,\n              duration_seconds as durationSeconds, status, created_at as createdAt,\n              updated_at as updatedAt\n       FROM sessions WHERE id = ?`\n    )\n    .get(id) as Session | undefined;\n}\n\n// --- Messages ---\n\nexport function addMessage(msg: Message): void {\n  getDb()\n    .prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO messages (id, session_id, role, getMessages(sessionId: string, limit = 100, offset = 0): Message[] {\n  return getDb()\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT id, session_id as sessionId, role, content, tokens, cost, created_at as createdAt\n       FROM messages WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?`\n    )\n    .all(sessionId, limit, offset) as Message[];\n}\n\n// --- Pins ---\n\nexport function addPin(pin: Pin): void {\n  getDb()\n    .prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO pins (id, session_id, type, target, mode, created_at)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`\n    )\n    .run(pin.id, pin.sessionId, pin.type, pin.target, pin.mode, pin.createdAt);\n}\n\nexport function removePinById(id: string): void {\n  getDb().prepare(\"DELETE FROM pins WHERE id = ?\").run(id);\n}\n\nexport function getPins(sessionId: string): Pin[] {\n  return getDb()\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT id, session_id as sessionId, type, target, mode, created_at as createdAt\n       FROM pins WHERE session_id = ?`\n    )\n    .all(sessionId) as Pin[];\n}\n\nexport function clearOneShots(sessionId: string): void {\n  getDb()\n    .prepare(\"DELETE FROM pins WHERE session_id = ? ...t, workerId, workerReady: true } : t\n        ),\n      }));\n    },\n\n    appendMessage: (tabId: string, msg: Message) => {\n      set((s: any) => ({\n        tabMessages: {\n          ...s.tabMessages,\n          [tabId]: [...(s.tabMessages[tabId] || []), msg],\n        },\n      }));\n    },\n\n    setStreamBuffer: (tabId: string, content: string) => {\n      set((s: any) => ({\n        tabStreamBuffer: { ...s.tabStreamBuffer, [tabId]: content },\n      }));\n    },\n\n    appendStreamBuffer: (tabId: string, chunk: string) => {\n      set((s: any) => ({\n        tabStreamBuffer: {\n          ...s.tabStreamBuffer,\n          [tabId]: (s.tabStreamBuffer[tabId] || \"\") + chunk,\n        },\n      }));\n    },\n\n    clearStreamBuffer: (tabId: string) => {\n      set((s: any) => ({\n        tabStreamBuffer: { ...s.tabStreamBuffer, [tabId]: \"\" },\n      }));\n    },\n\n    updateTabStatus: (tabId: string, data: Partial<StatusBarData>) => {\n      set((s: any) => ({\n        tabStatus: {\n          ...s.tabStatus,\n          [tabId]: { ...(s.tabStatus[tabId] || createEmptyStatus()), ...data },\n        },\n      }));\n    },\n\n    addCompactEvent: (tabId: string, event: CompactEvent) => {\n      set((s: any) => ({\n        tabCompactEvents: {\n          ...s.tabCompactEvents,\n          [tabId]: [...(s.tabCompactEvents[tabId] || []), event],\n        },\n      }));\n    },\n\n    renameTab: (tabId: string, name: string) => {\n      set((s: any) => ({\n        tabs: s.tabs.map((t: TabState) =>\n          t.id === tabId ? { ...t, name } : t\n        ),\n      }));\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createEmptyStatus(): StatusBarData {\n  return {\n    model: null,\n    totalTokens: 0,\n    contextTokens: 0,\n    contextMaxTokens: 1000000,\n    contextPercent: 0,\n    style: null,\n    sessionId: null,\n    durationSeconds: 0,\n    cost: 0,\n    gitBranch: null,\n    gitDiffStats: null,\n    compactedAt: null,\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/src/cli/index.ts",
  "content": "export interface CliArgs {\n  cwd: string;\n  session?: string;\n  model?: string;\n}\n\nexport function parseArgs(argv: string[]): CliArgs {\n  const args: CliArgs = { cwd: process.cwd() };\n\n  for (let i = 2; i < argv.length; i++) {\n    const arg = argv[i];\n    switch (arg) {\n      case \"--cwd\":\n        args.cwd = argv[++i] || process.cwd();\n        break;\n      case \"--session\":\n        args.session = argv[++i];\n        break;\n      case \"--model\":\n        args.model = argv[++i];\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return args;\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import React from \"react\";\nimport { Box, useStdout } from \"ink\";\n\ninterface LayoutProps {\n  sessionSidebar: React.ReactNode;\n  center: React.ReactNode;\n  skillsSidebar: React.ReactNode;\n  sidebarWidth?: number;\n}\n\nexport function Layout({\n  sessionSidebar,\n  center,\n  skillsSidebar,\n  sidebarWidth = 24,\n}: LayoutProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const { stdout } = useStdout();\n  const termHeight = stdout?.rows ?? 40;\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"row\" width=\"100%\" height={termHeight}>\n      <Box\n        width={sidebarWidth}\n        flexDirection=\"column\"\n        borderStyle=\"single\"\n        borderColor=\"gray\"\n      >\n        {sessionSidebar}\n      </Box>\n\n      <Box flexDirection=\"column\" flexGrow={1}>\n        {center}\n      </Box>\n\n      <Box\n        width={sidebarWidth}\n        flexDirection=\"column\"\n        borderStyle=\"single\"\n        borderColor=\"gray\"\n      >\n        {skillsSidebar}\n      </Box>\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/src/shell/components/TabBar.tsx",
  "content": "import React from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Text, useInput } from \"ink\";\nimport { useStore } from \"../store/index.js\";\n\nexport function TabBar(): React.ReactElement {\n  const tabs = useStore((s) => s.tabs);\n  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);\n  const setActiveTab = useStore((s) => s.setActiveTab);\n  const createTab = useStore((s) => s.createTab);\n  const closeTab = useStore((s) => s.closeTab);\n\n  useInput((input, key) => {\n    if (input === \"t\" && key.ctrl) {\n      createTab();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (input === \"w\" && key.ctrl && activeTabId) {\n      closeTab(activeTabId);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (key.ctrl && input >= \"1\" && input <= \"9\") {\n      const idx = parseInt(input, 10) - 1;\n      if (tabs[idx]) {\n        setActiveTab(tabs[idx].id);\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (key.tab && !key.shift && tabs.length > 1) {\n      const currentIdx = tabs.findIndex((t) => t.id === activeTabId);\n      const nextIdx = (currentIdx + 1) % tabs.length;\n      setActiveTab(tabs[nextIdx].id);\n    }\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"row\" borderStyle=\"single\" borderColor=\"gray\" borderBottom={false}>\n      {tabs.map((tab) => (\n        <Box key={tab.id} paddingX={1}>\n          <Text\n            color={tab.id === activeTabId ? \"cyan\" : \"gray\"}\n            bold={tab.id === activeTabId}\n          >\n            {tab.index}: {tab.name}\n          </Text>\n        </Box>\n      ))}\n      <Box paddingX={1}>\n        <Text color=\"green\">[+]</Text>\n      </Box>\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/src/shell/components/ChatView.tsx",
  "content": "import React from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Static, Text } from \"ink\";\nimport { useStore } from \"../store/index.js\";\nimport type { Message } from \"../../shared/types.js\";\n\nfunction MessageBubble({ msg }: { msg: Message }): React.ReactElement {\n  const color = msg.role === \"user\" ? \"green\" : msg.role === \"assistant\" ? \"white\" : \"gray\";\n  const prefix = msg.role === \"user\" ? \"> \" : \"  \";\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"column\" marginBottom={1}>\n      <Text color={color} dimColor={msg.role === \"system\"}>\n        {prefix}\n        {msg.content}\n      </Text>\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function ChatView(): React.ReactElement {\n  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);\n  const messages = useStore((s) =>\n    activeTabId ? s.tabMessages[activeTabId] || [] : []\n  );\n  const streamBuffer = useStore((s) =>\n    activeTabId ? s.tabStreamBuffer[activeTabId] || \"\" : \"\"\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"column\" flexGrow={1} paddingX={1}>\n      <Static items={messages}>\n        {(msg) => <MessageBubble key={msg.id} msg={msg} />}\n      </Static>\n\n      {streamBuffer.length > 0 && (\n        <Box>\n          <Text color=\"white\">  {streamBuffer}</Text>\n          <Text color=\"cyan\">|</Text>\n        </Box>\n      )}\n\n      {messages.length === 0 && streamBuffer.length === 0 && (\n        <Box justifyContent=\"center\" alignItems=\"center\" flexGrow={1}>\n          <Text color=\"gray\">Start a conversation...</Text>\n        </Box>\n      )}\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "import React, { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Text } from \"ink\";\nimport TextInput from \"ink-text-input\";\n\ninterface InputBarProps {\n  onSubmit: (text: string) => void;\n  disabled?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport function InputBar({ onSubmit, disabled = false }: InputBarProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const [value, setValue] = useState(\"\");\n\n  function handleSubmit(text: string): void {\n    const trimmed = text.trim();\n    if (!trimmed) return;\n    onSubmit(trimmed);\n    setValue(\"\");\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <Box borderStyle=\"single\" borderColor=\"gray\" borderTop={false} paddingX={1}>\n      <Text color=\"green\">&gt; </Text>\n      <TextInput\n        value={value}\n        onChange={setValue}\n        onSubmit={handleSubmit}\n        placeholder={disabled ? \"Waiting for response...\" : \"Type a message...\"}\n        focus={!disabled}\n      />\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "import React from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Text } from \"ink\";\nimport type { Pin } from \"../../shared/types.js\";\n\ninterface ContextBarProps {\n  pins: Pin[];\n  onRemovePin: (pinId: string) => void;\n}\n\nexport function ContextBar({ pins, onRemovePin }: ContextBarProps): React.ReactElement {\n  if (pins.length === 0) return <></>;\n\n  const stickyPins = pins.filter((p) => p.mode === \"sticky\");\n  const oneshotPins = pins.filter((p) => p.mode === \"prompt\");\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"row\" flexWrap=\"wrap\" paddingX={1} borderStyle=\"single\" borderColor=\"gray\" borderBottom={false}>\n      {stickyPins.map((pin) => (\n        <Box key={pin.id} marginRight={1}>\n          <Text color=\"cyan\">\n            [pin] {pin.target}\n          </Text>\n        </Box>\n      ))}\n      {oneshotPins.map((pin) => (\n        <Box key={pin.id} marginRight={1}>\n          <Text color=\"yellow\">\n            [1x] {pin.target}\n          </Text>\n        </Box>\n      ))}\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}
