{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/src/shell/components/StatusBar.tsx",
  "content": "import React from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Text } from \"ink\";\nimport { useStore } from \"../store/index.js\";\nimport type { StatusBarData, StatusBarField } from \"../../shared/types.js\";\nimport { config } from \"../../shared/config.js\";\n\nfunction formatDuration(seconds: number): string {\n  if (seconds < 60) return `${seconds}s`;\n  const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);\n  const s = seconds % 60;\n  return s > 0 ? `${m}m${s}s` : `${m}m`;\n}\n\nfunction formatCost(cost: number): string {\n  return `$${cost.toFixed(2)}`;\n}\n\nfunction formatTokens(n: number): string {\n  if (n >= 1000000) return `${(n / 1000000).toFixed(1)}M`;\n  if (n >= 1000) return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(1)}k`;\n  return `${n}`;\n}\n\nfunction renderField(\n  key: string,\n  _label: string,\n  data: StatusBarData\n): React.ReactElement | null {\n  switch (key) {\n    case \"model\":\n      return data.model ? <Text color=\"green\">{data.model}</Text> : null;\n    case \"totalTokens\":\n      return <Text color=\"yellow\">Total: {formatTokens(data.totalTokens)}</Text>;\n    case \"contextTokens\":\n      return <Text color=\"yellow\">Ctx: {formatTokens(data.contextTokens)}</Text>;\n    case \"contextPercent\": {\n      const pct = data.contextPercent;\n      const color = pct > 80 ? \"red\" : pct > 50 ? \"yellow\" : \"green\";\n      return <Text color={color}>Ctx: {pct.toFixed(1)}%</Text>;\n    }\n    case \"cost\":\n      return <Text color=\"cyan\">{formatCost(data.cost)}</Text>;\n    case \"durationSeconds\":\n      return <Text color=\"magenta\">{formatDuration(data.durationSeconds)}</Text>;\n    case \"gitBranch\":\n      return data.gitBranch ? (\n        <Text color=\"yellow\">@ {data.gitBranch}</Text>\n      ) : (\n        <Text color=\"gray\">@ no git</Text>\n      );\n    case \"gitDiffStats\":\n      if (!data.gitDiffStats) return null;\n      return (\n        <Text>\n          <Text color=\"green\">+{data.gitDiffStats.added}</Text>\n          <Text> </Text>\n          <Text color=\"red\">-{data.gitDiffStats.removed}</Text>\n          <Text> </Text>\n          <Text color=\"yellow\">~{data.gitDiffStats.modified}</Text>\n        </Text>\n      );\n    case \"style\":\n      return data.style ? <Text color=\"white\">Style: {data.style}</Text> : null;\n    case \"sessionId\":\n      return data.sessionId ? <Text color=\"gray\">{data.sessionId.slice(0, 8)}</Text> : null;\n    default:\n      return null;\n  }\n}\n\ninterface StatusBarProps {\n  fields?: StatusBarField[];\n}\n\nexport function StatusBar({ fields }: StatusBarProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);\n  const data = useStore((s) =>\n    activeTabId ? s.tabStatus[activeTabId] : null\n  );\n  const compactEvents = useStore((s) =>\n    activeTabId ? s.tabCompactEvents[activeTabId] || [] : []\n  );\n\n  const enabledFields = (fields || config.defaultStatusBarFields).filter(\n    (f) => f.enabled\n  );\n\n  if (!data) {\n    return (\n      <Box paddingX={1}>\n        <Text color=\"gray\">No active session</Text>\n      </Box>\n    );\n  }\n\n  const lastCompact = compactEvents[compactEvents.length - 1];\n\n  return (\n    <Box paddingX={1} flexDirection=\"row\" gap={1}>\n      {enabledFields.map((field) => {\n        const rendered = renderField(field.key, field.label, data);\n        if (!rendered) return null;\n        return (\n          <React.Fragment key={field.key}>\n            {rendered}\n            <Text color=\"gray\"> | </Text>\n          </React.Fragment>\n        );\n      })}\n      {lastCompact && <Text color=\"red\">* compacted</Text>}\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

\"./shell/components/TabBar.js\";\nimport { ChatView } from \"./shell/components/ChatView.js\";\nimport { InputBar } from \"./shell/components/InputBar.js\";\nimport { StatusBar } from \"./shell/components/StatusBar.js\";\nimport { ContextBar } from \"./shell/components/ContextBar.js\";\nimport { useStore } from \"./shell/store/index.js\";\nimport type { CliArgs } from \"./cli/index.js\";\n\ninterface AppProps {\n  args: CliArgs;\n}\n\nexport function App({ args }: AppProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const createTab = useStore((s) => s.createTab);\n  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);\n  const [initialized, setInitialized] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!initialized) {\n      createTab(\"New Session\");\n      setInitialized(true);\n    }\n  }, [initialized, createTab]);\n\n  function handleSubmit(text: string): void {\n    if (!activeTabId) return;\n    const { appendMessage } = useStore.getState();\n    appendMessage(activeTabId, {\n      id: crypto.randomUUID(),\n      sessionId: activeTabId,\n      role: \"user\",\n      content: text,\n      tokens: 0,\n      cost: 0,\n      createdAt: Date.now(),\n    });\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <Layout\n      sessionSidebar={\n        <Box flexDirection=\"column\" padding={1}>\n          <Text bold color=\"cyan\">Sessions</Text>\n          <Text color=\"gray\">Phase 3</Text>\n        </Box>\n      }\n      center={\n        <Box flexDirection=\"column\" flexGrow={1}>\n          <TabBar />\n          <ChatView />\n          <ContextBar pins={[]} onRemovePin={() => {}} />\n          <InputBar onSubmit={handleSubmit} />\n          <StatusBar />\n        </Box>\n      }\n      skillsSidebar={\n        <Box flexDirection=\"column\" padding={1}>\n          <Text bold color=\"cyan\">Skills</Text>\n          <Text color=\"gray\">Phase 4</Text>\n        </Box>\n      }\n    />\n  );\n}\n"
}

71	export function createIPCServer(
72	  sockPath: string,
73	  onMessage: (msg: IPCMessage, socket: net.Socket) => void
74	): net.Server {
75	  const server = net.createServer((socket) => {
76	    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();
77	    socket.on("data", (data) => {
78	      for (const msg of decoder.feed(data)) {
79	        onMessage(msg, socket);
80	      }
81	    });
82	  });
83	
84	  server.listen(sockPath);
85	  return server;
86	}
87	
88	export function connectIPC(
89	  sockPath: string,
90	  onMessage: (msg: IPCMessage) => void
91	): Promise<net.Socket> {
92	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
93	    const socket = net.createConnection(sockPath, () => {
94	      const decoder = new MessageDecoder();
95	      socket.on("data", (data) => {
96	        for (const msg of decoder.feed(data)) {
97	          onMessage(msg);
98	        }
99	      });

from \"node:os\";\nimport { v4 as uuid } from \"uuid\";\n\nfunction createTestDb(): Database.Database {\n  const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), \"clui-test-\"));\n  const dbPath = path.join(tmpDir, \"test.db\");\n  const db = new Database(dbPath);\n  db.pragma(\"journal_mode = WAL\");\n  db.pragma(\"foreign_keys = ON\");\n\n  const migrationPath = path.join(\n    import.meta.dirname,\n    \"../../db/migrations/001_init.sql\"\n  );\n  const sql = fs.readFileSync(migrationPath, \"utf-8\");\n  db.exec(sql);\n\n  return db;\n}\n\ndescribe(\"database\", () => {\n  let db: Database.Database;\n\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    db = createTestDb();\n  });\n\n  afterEach(() => {\n    db.close();\n  });\n\n  it(\"creates all tables\", () => {\n    const tables = db\n      .prepare(\"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'\")\n      .all()\n      .map((r: any) => r.name);\n    expect(tables).toContain(\"sessions\");\n    expect(tables).toContain(\"messages\");\n    expect(tables).toContain(\"pins\");\n    expect(tables).toContain(\"snapshots\");\n    expect(tables).toContain(\"settings\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"inserts and retrieves a session\", () => {\n    const id = uuid();\n    const now = Date.now();\n    db.prepare(\n      `INSERT INTO sessions (id, name, status, created_at, updated_at)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`\n    ).run(id, \"test session\", \"active\", now, now);\n\n    const row = db\n      .prepare(\"SELECT * FROM sessions WHERE id = ?\")\n      .get(id) as any;\n    expect(row.name).toBe(\"test session\");\n    expect(row.status).toBe(\"active\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"cascades message delete on session delete\", () => {\n    const sessionId = uuid();\n    const msgId = uuid();\n    const now = Date.now();\n\n    db.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO sessions (id, name, status, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\"\n    ).run(sessionId, \"s\", \"active\", now, now);\n\n    db.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO messages (id, session_id, role, content, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\"\n    ).run(msgId, sessionId, \"user\", \"hello\", now);\n\n    db.prepare(\"DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id = ?\").run(sessionId);\n\n    const msg = db.prepare(\"SELECT * FROM messages WHERE id = ?\").get(msgId);\n    expect(msg).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  it(\"stores and retrieves settings as JSON\", () => {\n    db.prepare(\"INSERT INTO settings (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)\").run(\n      \"theme\",\n      JSON.stringify({ dark: true })\n    );\n\n    const row = db\n      .prepare(\"SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?\")\n      .get(\"theme\") as any;\n    expect(JSON.parse(row.value)).toEqual({ dark: true });\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/test/shared/ipc.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, it, expect } from \"vitest\";\nimport { encodeMessage, MessageDecoder } from \"../../src/shared/ipc.js\";\nimport type { IPCMessage } from \"../../src/shared/ipc.js\";\n\ndescribe(\"IPC framing\", () => {\n  it(\"encodes and decodes a single message\", () => {\n    const msg: IPCMessage = { type: \"ready\", workerId: \"w1\" };\n    const encoded = encodeMessage(msg);\n    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();\n    const decoded = decoder.feed(encoded);\n    expect(decoded).toEqual([msg]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"handles multiple messages in one buffer\", () => {\n    const msg1: IPCMessage = { type: \"ready\", workerId: \"w1\" };\n    const msg2: IPCMessage = { type: \"stream_chunk\", content: \"hello\" };\n    const buf = Buffer.concat([encodeMessage(msg1), encodeMessage(msg2)]);\n    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();\n    const decoded = decoder.feed(buf);\n    expect(decoded).toEqual([msg1, msg2]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"handles partial messages across feeds\", () => {\n    const msg: IPCMessage = { type: \"stream_chunk\", content: \"world\" };\n    const full = encodeMessage(msg);\n    const decoder = new MessageDecoder();\n\n    const mid = Math.floor(full.length / 2);\n    const part1 = decoder.feed(full.subarray(0, mid));\n    expect(part1).toEqual([]);\n\n    const part2 = decoder.feed(full.subarray(mid));\n    expect(part2).toEqual([msg]);\n  });\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/src/shell/hooks/useWorkerManager.ts",
  "content": "import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from \"react\";\nimport { fork, type ChildProcess } from \"node:child_process\";\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nimport net from \"node:net\";\nimport { v4 as uuid } from \"uuid\";\nimport { useStore } from \"../store/index.js\";\nimport {\n  createIPCServer,\n  sendMessage,\n  socketPath,\n  type IPCMessage,\n  type WorkerToShellMessage,\n  type ShellToWorkerMessage,\n} from \"../../shared/ipc.js\";\nimport { config, ensureDirs } from \"../../shared/config.js\";\nimport type { Pin } from \"../../shared/types.js\";\n\ninterface WorkerHandle {\n  process: ChildProcess;\n  socket: net.Socket | null;\n  server: net.Server;\n  workerId: string;\n  tabId: string;\n}\n\nexport function useWorkerManager(cwd: string) {\n  const workersRef = useRef<Map<string, WorkerHandle>>(new Map());\n\n  const spawnWorker = useCallback(\n    (tabId: string): string => {\n      ensureDirs();\n      const workerId = uuid();\n      const sockPath = socketPath(workerId);\n\n      // Clean up stale socket\n      try {\n        fs.unlinkSync(sockPath);\n      } catch {}\n\n      // Create IPC server for this worker\n      const server = createIPCServer(sockPath, (msg, socket) => {\n        const handle = workersRef.current.get(tabId);\n        if (handle && !handle.socket) {\n          handle.socket = socket;\n        }\n        handleWorkerMessage(tabId, msg as WorkerToShellMessage);\n      });\n\n      // Fork worker process\n      const workerProcess = fork(config.workerEntry, [workerId, cwd], {\n        stdio: \"pipe\",\n        env: { ...process.env },\n      });\n\n      workerProcess.on(\"exit\", (code) => {\n        cleanupWorker(tabId);\n        if (code !== 0) {\n          useStore.getState().appendMessage(tabId, {\n            id: uuid(),\n            sessionId: tabId,\n            role: \"system\",\n            content: `Worker exited with code ${code}`,\n            tokens: 0,\n            cost: 0,\n            createdAt: Date.now(),\n          });\n        }\n      });\n\n      workersRef.current.set(tabId, {\n        process: workerProcess,\n        socket: null,\n        server,\n        workerId,\n        tabId,\n      });\n\n      return workerId;\n    },\n    [cwd]\n  );\n\n  function handleWorkerMessage(tabId: string, msg: WorkerToShellMessage): void {\n    const store = useStore.getState();\n\n    switch (msg.type) {\n      case \"ready\":\n        store.setWorkerReady(tabId, msg.workerId);\n        break;\n\n      case \"stream_chunk\":\n        store.appendStreamBuffer(tabId, msg.content);\n        break;\n\n      case \"message_complete\":\n        store.clearStreamBuffer(tabId);\n        store.appendMessage(tabId, {\n          id: uuid(),\n          sessionId: tabId,\n          role: msg.role as any,\n          content: msg.content,\n          tokens: msg.tokens,\n          cost: msg.cost,\n          createdAt: Date.now(),\n        });\n        break;\n\n      case \"status_update\":\n        store.updateTabStatus(tabId, msg.data);\n        break;\n\n      case \"context_compacted\":\n        store.addCompactEvent(tabId, msg.event);\n        break;\n\n      case \"error\":\n        store.appendMessage(tabId, {\n          id: uuid(),\n          sessionId: tabId,\n          role: \"system\",\n          content: `Error: ${msg.message}`,\n          tokens: 0,\n          cost: 0,\n          createdAt: Date.now(),\n        });\n        break;\n    }\n  }\n\n  function sendToWorker(tabId: string, msg: ShellToWorkerMessage): void {\n    const handle = workersRef.current.get(tabId);\n    if (handle?.socket) {\n      sendMessage(handle.socket, msg);\n    }\n  }\n\n  function killWorker(tabId: string): void {\n    const handle = workersRef.current.get(tabId);\n    if (handle) {\n      sendToWorker(tabId, { type: \"shutdown\" });\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        handle.process.kill(\"SIGTERM\");\n        cleanupWorker(tabId);\n      }, 2000);\n    }\n  }\n\n  function cleanupWorker(tabId: string): void {\n    const handle = workersRef.current.get(tabId);\n    if (handle) {\n      handle.socket?.destroy();\n      handle.server.close();\n      try {\n        fs.unlinkSync(socketPath(handle.workerId));\n      } catch {}\n      workersRef.current.delete(tabId);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Cleanup all workers on unmount\n  useEffect(() => {\n    return () => {\n      for (const [tabId] of workersRef.current) {\n        killWorker(tabId);\n      }\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return {\n    spawnWorker,\n    sendToWorker,\n    killWorker,\n    sendPrompt: (tabId: string, prompt: string, pins: Pin[]) => {\n      const store = useStore.getState();\n      store.appendMessage(tabId, {\n        id: uuid(),\n        sessionId: tabId,\n        role: \"user\",\n        content: prompt,\n        tokens: 0,\n        cost: 0,\n        createdAt: Date.now(),\n      });\n      sendToWorker(tabId, { type: \"send_prompt\", prompt, pins });\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
}

\"./shell/components/TabBar.js\";\nimport { ChatView } from \"./shell/components/ChatView.js\";\nimport { InputBar } from \"./shell/components/InputBar.js\";\nimport { StatusBar } from \"./shell/components/StatusBar.js\";\nimport { ContextBar } from \"./shell/components/ContextBar.js\";\nimport { useStore } from \"./shell/store/index.js\";\nimport { useWorkerManager } from \"./shell/hooks/useWorkerManager.js\";\nimport type { CliArgs } from \"./cli/index.js\";\n\ninterface AppProps {\n  args: CliArgs;\n}\n\nexport function App({ args }: AppProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const createTab = useStore((s) => s.createTab);\n  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);\n  const tabs = useStore((s) => s.tabs);\n  const [initialized, setInitialized] = useState(false);\n\n  const { spawnWorker, sendPrompt, killWorker } = useWorkerManager(args.cwd);\n\n  // Create initial tab and spawn its worker\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!initialized) {\n      const tab = createTab(\"New Session\");\n      spawnWorker(tab.id);\n      setInitialized(true);\n    }\n  }, [initialized, createTab, spawnWorker]);\n\n  // Spawn worker when new tab is created (watch for tabs without workers)\n  useEffect(() => {\n    for (const tab of tabs) {\n      if (!tab.workerId && !tab.workerReady) {\n        spawnWorker(tab.id);\n      }\n    }\n  }, [tabs, spawnWorker]);\n\n  function handleSubmit(text: string): void {\n    if (!activeTabId) return;\n    sendPrompt(activeTabId, text, []);\n  }\n\n  function handleCloseTab(tabId: string): void {\n 2937	// src/shell/store/skills.ts

2939	import type { Skill, SkillTier } from "../../shared/types.js";
2940	
2941	export interface SkillsSlice {
2942	  skills: Skill[];
2943	  expandedTiers: Record<SkillTier, boolean>;
2944	  setSkills: (skills: Skill[]) => void;
2945	  toggleTier: (tier: SkillTier) => void;
2946	  getSkillsByTier: (tier: SkillTier) => Skill[];
2947	}
2948	
2949	export function createSkillsSlice(
2950	  set: (fn: (state: any) => any) => void,
2951	  get: () => any
2952	): SkillsSlice {
2953	  return {
2954	    skills: [],
2955	    expandedTiers: { repo: true, user: false, global: false },
2956	
2957	    setSkills: (skills: Skill[]) => set(() => ({ skills })),
2958	
2959	    toggleTier: (tier: SkillTier) => {
2960	      set((s: any) => ({
2961	        expandedTiers: {
2962	          ...s.expandedTiers,
2963	          [tier]: !s.expandedTiers[tier],
2964	        },
2965	      }));
2966	    },
2967	
2968	    getSkillsByTier: (tier: SkillTier) => {
2969	      return get().skills.filter((s: Skill) => s.tier === tier);
2970	    },
2971	  };
2972	}

3011	// src/shell/hooks/useSkillDiscovery.ts
3012	
3013	import { useEffect, useCallback } from "react";
3014	import fs from "node:fs";
3015	import path from "node:path";
3016	import { useStore } from "../store/index.js";
3017	import { config } from "../../shared/config.js";
3018	import type { Skill, SkillTier } from "../../shared/types.js";
3019	
3020	function scanSkillDir(dir: string, tier: SkillTier): Skill[] {
3021	  const skills: Skill[] = [];
3022	
3023	  try {
3024	    if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return skills;
3025	
3026	    const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
3027	    for (const entry of entries) {
3028	      if (entry.isFile() && entry.name.endsWith(".md")) {
3029	        const filePath = path.join(dir, entry.name);
3030	        const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf-8");
3031	
3032	        // Parse frontmatter for name/description
3033	        const frontmatter = parseFrontmatter(content);
3034	        const name = frontmatter.name || entry.name.replace(/\.md$/, "");
3035	        const description = frontmatter.description || "";
3036	
3037	        skills.push({
3038	          name,
3039	          description,
3040	          tier,
3041	          filePath,
3042	          overriddenBy: null,
3043	        });
3044	      } else if (entry.isDirectory()) {
3045	        // Check for index.md or skill.md inside directory
3046	        const indexPath = path.join(dir, entry.name, "index.md");
3047	        const skillPath = path.join(dir, entry.name, "skill.md");
3048	        const targetPath = fs.existsSync(indexPath)
3049	          ? indexPath
3050	          : fs.existsSync(skillPath)
3051	            ? skillPath
3052	            : null;
3053	
3054	        if (targetPath) {
3055	          const content = fs.readFileSync(targetPath, "utf-8");
3056	          const frontmatter = parseFrontmatter(content);
3057	          skills.push({
3058	            name: frontmatter.name || entry.name,
3059	            description: frontmatter.description || "",
3060	            tier,
3061	            filePath: targetPath,
3062	            overriddenBy: null,
3063	          });
3064	        }
3065	      }
3066	    }
3067	  } catch {
3068	    // Directory doesn't exist or isn't readable
3069	  }
3070	
3071	  return skills;
3072	}
3073	
3074	function parseFrontmatter(content: string): Record<string, string> {
3075	  const match = content.match(/^---\n([\s\S]*?)\n---/);
3076	  if (!match) return {};
3077	
3078	  const result: Record<string, string> = {};
3079	  for (const line of match[1].split("\n")) {
3080	    const [key, ...rest] = line.split(":");
3081	    if (key && rest.length > 0) {
3082	      result[key.trim()] = rest.join(":").trim();
3083	    }
3084	  }
3085	  return result;
3086	}
3087	
3088	function markOverrides(skills: Skill[]): Skill[] {
3089	  const nameToHighestTier = new Map<string, SkillTier>();
3090	  const tierPriority: Record<SkillTier, number> = {
3091	    repo: 0,
3092	    user: 1,
3093	    global: 2,
3094	  };
3095	
3096	  // Find highest priority tier for each skill name
3097	  for (const skill of skills) {
3098	    const existing = nameToHighestTier.get(skill.name);
3099	    if (!existing || tierPriority[skill.tier] < tierPriority[existing]) {
```

3113	
3114	export function useSkillDiscovery(cwd: string): {
3115	  refresh: () => void;
3116	} {
3117	  const setSkills = useStore((s) => s.setSkills);
3118	
3119	  const refresh = useCallback(() => {
3120	    const repoSkills = scanSkillDir(config.skillDirs.repo(cwd), "repo");
3121	    const userSkills = scanSkillDir(config.skillDirs.user, "user");
3122	    const globalSkills = scanSkillDir(config.skillDirs.global, "global");
3123	
3124	    const all = markOverrides([...repoSkills, ...userSkills, ...globalSkills]);
3125	    setSkills(all);
3126	  }, [cwd, setSkills]);
3127	
3128	  useEffect(() => {
3129	    refresh();
3130	  }, [refresh]);
3131	
3132	  return { refresh };
3133	}

3177	function SkillRow({

3197	
3198	export function SkillsSidebar({
3199	  visible,
3200	  onPinSkill,

3278	```typescript
3279	// src/shell/store/settings.ts
3280	
3281	import type { Pin, PinMode, PinType, StatusBarField } from "../../shared/types.js";
3282	import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
3283	import { config } from "../../shared/config.js";
3284	
3285	export interface SettingsSlice {
3286	  pins: Record<string, Pin[]>; // keyed by tabId
3287	  statusBarFields: StatusBarField[];
3288	
3289	  addPin: (tabId: string, type: PinType, target: string, mode: PinMode) => void;
3290	  removePin: (tabId: string, pinId: string) => void;
3291	  clearOneShots: (tabId: string) => void;
3292	  getPins: (tabId: string) => Pin[];
3293	
3294	  setStatusBarFields: (fields: StatusBarField[]) => void;
3295	  toggleStatusBarField: (key: string) => void;
3296	}
3297	
3298	export function createSettingsSlice(
3299	  set: (fn: (state: any) => any) => void,
3300	  get: () => any
3301	): SettingsSlice {
3302	  return {
3303	    pins: {},
3304	    statusBarFields: [...config.defaultStatusBarFields],
3305	
3306	    addPin: (tabId: string, type: PinType, target: string, mode: PinMode) => {
3307	      const pin: Pin = {
3308	        id: uuid(),
3309	        sessionId: tabId,
3310	        type,
3311	        target,
3312	        mode,
3313	        createdAt: Date.now(),
3314	      };
3315	      set((s: any) => ({
3316	        pins: {
3317	          ...s.pins,
3318	          [tabId]: [...(s.pins[tabId] || []), pin],
3319	        },
3320	      }));
3321	    },
3322	
3323	    removePin: (tabId: string, pinId: string) => {
3324	      set((s: any) => ({
3325	        pins: {
3326	          ...s.pins,
3327	          [tabId]: (s.pins[tabId] || []).filter((p: Pin) => p.id !== pinId),
3328	        },
3329	      }));
3330	    },
3331	
3332	    clearOneShots: (tabId: string) => {
3333	      set((s: any) => ({
3334	        pins: {
3335	          ...s.pins,
3336	          [tabId]: (s.pins[tabId] || []).filter((p: Pin) => p.mode !== "prompt"),
3337	        },
3338	      }));
3339	    },
3340	
3341	    getPins: (tabId: string) => {
3342	      return get().pins[tabId] || [];
3343	    },
3344	
3345	    setStatusBarFields: (fields: StatusBarField[]) =>
3346	      set(() => ({ statusBarFields: fields })),
3347	
3348	    toggleStatusBarField: (key: string) => {
3349	      set((s: any) => ({
3350	        statusBarFields: s.statusBarFields.map((f: StatusBarField) =>
3351	          f.key === key ? { ...f, enabled: !f.enabled } : f
3352	        ),
3353	      }));
3354	    },
3355	  };
3356	}

3490	- [ ] **Step 1: Write context manager**
3491	
3492	```typescript
3493	// src/worker/context-manager.ts
3494	
3495	import fs from "node:fs";
3496	import path from "node:path";
3497	import { v4 as uuid } from "uuid";
3498	import { config } from "../shared/config.js";
3499	
3500	export class ContextManager {
3501	  private cwd: string;
3502	
3503	  constructor(cwd: string) {
3504	    this.cwd = cwd;
3505	  }
3506	
3507	  async createSnapshot(
3508	    sessionId: string,
3509	    messages: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>,
3510	    pins: Array<{ type: string; target: string; mode: string }>
3511	  ): Promise<{ filePath: string; summary: string }> {
3512	    // Build a summary from messages (last 20 messages)
3513	    const recent = messages.slice(-20);
3514	    const conversationText = recent
3515	      .map((m) => `${m.role}: ${m.content.slice(0, 200)}`)
3516	      .join("\n");
3517	
3518	    const summary = this.buildSummary(conversationText);
3519	
3520	    // Save to memory file
3521	    const snapshotDir = config.snapshotDir(this.cwd);
3522	    fs.mkdirSync(snapshotDir, { recursive: true });
3523	
3524	    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
3525	    const fileName = `snapshot-${timestamp}.md`;
3526	    const filePath = path.join(snapshotDir, fileName);
3527	
3528	    const content = `---
3529	name: Session Snapshot ${timestamp}
3530	description: Conversation snapshot from clui session ${sessionId}
3531	type: project
3532	---
3533	
3534	## Summary
3535	
3536	${summary}
3537	
3538	## Context State
3539	
3540	**Active pins:**
3541	${pins.map((p) => `- [${p.mode}] ${p.type}: ${p.target}`).join("\n") || "None"}
3542	
3543	## Key Messages
3544	
3545	${recent.map((m) => `**${m.role}:** ${m.content.slice(0, 500)}`).join("\n\n")}
3546	`;
3547	
3548	    fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, "utf-8");
3549	
3550	    return { filePath, summary };
3551	  }
3552	
3553	  private buildSummary(conversationText: string): string {
3554	    // Simple extractive summary — take first and last user messages
3555	    const lines = conversationText.split("\n");
3556	    const userLines = lines.filter((l) => l.startsWith("user:"));
3557	
3558	    if (userLines.length === 0) return "Empty conversation.";
3559	    if (userLines.length === 1) return userLines[0].replace("user: ", "");
3560	
3561	    return `Started with: "${userLines[0].replace("user: ", "").slice(0, 100)}"\nEnded with: "${userLines[userLines.length - 1].replace("user: ", "").slice(0, 100)}"`;
3562	  }
3563	}
3564	```

nameToHighestTier.get(skill.name)!;\n    if (skill.tier !== highestTier) {\n      return { ...skill, overriddenBy: highestTier };\n    }\n    return skill;\n  });\n}\n\nexport function useSkillDiscovery(cwd: string): {\n  refresh: () => void;\n} {\n  const setSkills = useStore((s) => s.setSkills);\n\n  const refresh = useCallback(() => {\n    const repoSkills = scanSkillDir(config.skillDirs.repo(cwd), \"repo\");\n    const userSkills = scanSkillDir(config.skillDirs.user, \"user\");\n    const globalSkills = scanSkillDir(config.skillDirs.global, \"global\");\n\n    const all = markOverrides([...repoSkills, ...userSkills, ...globalSkills]);\n    setSkills(all);\n  }, [cwd, setSkills]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    refresh();\n  },   "content": "import React, { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Text, useInput } from \"ink\";\nimport TextInput from \"ink-text-input\";\nimport { useStore } from \"../store/index.js\";\nimport type { Session } from \"../../shared/types.js\";\n\ninterface SessionSidebarProps {\n  onOpenSession: (session: Session) => void;\n  visible: boolean;\n}\n\nconst COLORS = [\"red\", \"green\", \"yellow\", \"blue\", \"magenta\", \"cyan\", \"white\"];\n\nfunction formatDate(ts: number): string {\n  const d = new Date(ts);\n  const month = (d.getMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, \"0\");\n  const day = d.getDate().toString().padStart(2, \"0\");\n  return `${month}/${day}`;\n}\n\nfunction formatCost(cost: number): string {\n  if (cost === 0) return \"\";\n  return `$${cost.toFixed(2)}`;\n}\n\nexport function SessionSidebar({\n  onOpenSession,\n  visible,\n}: SessionSidebarProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const sessionFilter = useStore((s) => s.sessionFilter);\n  const setSessionFilter = useStore((s) => s.setSessionFilter);\n  const filteredSessions = useStore((s) => s.filteredSessions);\n  const updateSessionColor = useStore((s) => s.updateSessionColor);\n  const updateSessionName = useStore((s) => s.updateSessionName);\n\n  const [selectedIdx, setSelectedIdx] = useState(0);\n  const [renaming, setRenaming] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [renameValue, setRenameValue] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const filtered = filteredSessions();\n\n  useInput(\n    (input, key) => {\n      if (!visible) return;\n\n      if (key.upArrow) {\n        setSelectedIdx((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1));\n        return;\n      }\n      if (key.downArrow) {\n        setSelectedIdx((i) => Math.min(filtered.length - 1, i + 1));\n        return;\n      }\n      if (key.return && filtered[selectedIdx]) {\n        if (renaming) return;\n        onOpenSession(filtered[selectedIdx]);\n        return;\n      }\n      if (input === \"r\" && !renaming && filtered[selectedIdx]) {\n        setRenaming(filtered[selectedIdx].id);\n        setRenameValue(filtered[selectedIdx].name);\n        return;\n      }\n      if (input === \"c\" && filtered[selectedIdx]) {\n        const session = filtered[selectedIdx];\n        const currentIdx = COLORS.indexOf(session.color || \"\");\n        const nextColor = COLORS[(currentIdx + 1) % COLORS.length];\n        updateSessionColor(session.id, nextColor);\n        return;\n      }\n    },\n    { isActive: visible }\n  );\n\n  function handleRenameSubmit(value: string): void {\n    if (renaming && value.trim()) {\n      updateSessionName(renaming, value.trim());\n    }\n    setRenaming(null);\n    setRenameValue(\"\");\n  }\n\n  if (!visible) return <></>;\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"column\" padding={1}>\n      <Text bold color=\"cyan\">Sessions</Text>\n\n      <Box marginY={1}>\n        <Text color=\"gray\">/ </Text>\n        <TextInput\n          value={sessionFilter}\n          onChange={setSessionFilter}\n          placeholder=\"Filter...\"\n          focus={visible && !renaming}\n        />\n      </Box>\n\n      {filtered.map((session, idx) => {\n        const isSelected = idx === selectedIdx;\n        const isRenaming = renaming === session.id;\n\n        return (\n          <Box key={session.id} flexDirection=\"row\">\n            <Text color={session.color || \"white\"}>\n              {isSelected ? \"> \" : \"  \"}o{\" \"}\n            </Text>\n            {isRenaming ? (\n              <TextInput\n                value={renameValue}\n                onChange={setRenameValue}\n                onSubmit={handleRenameSubmit}\n                focus={true}\n              />\n            ) : (\n              <Text\n                color={isSelected ? \"white\" : \"gray\"}\n                bold={isSelected}\n              >\n                {session.name}\n              </Text>\n            )}\n            <Text color=\"gray\">\n              {\" \"}\n              {formatDate(session.updatedAt)}\n              {formatCost(session.totalCost) && ` ${formatCost(session.totalCost)}`}\n            </Text>\n          </Box>\n        );\n      })}\n\n      {filtered.length === 0 && (\n        <Text color=\"gray\">No sessions found</Text>\n      )}\n\n      <Box marginTop={1}>\n        <Text color=\"gray\" dimColor>\n          up/dn nav | Enter open | r rename | c color\n        </Text>\n      </Box>\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

\"gray\" : \"white\"} dimColor={isOverridden}>\n        {isOverridden ? \"[ ]\" : \"[x]\"} {skill.name}\n      </Text>\n      {!isOverridden && (\n        <Text color=\"gray\"> [pin]</Text>\n      )}\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function SkillsSidebar({\n  visible,\n  onPinSkill,\n}: SkillsSidebarProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const skills = useStore((s) => s.skills);\n  const expandedTiers = useStore((s) => s.expandedTiers);\n\n  if (!visible) return <></>;\n\n  const tiers: SkillTier[] = [\"repo\", \"user\", \"global\"];\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"column\" padding={1}>\n      <Text bold color=\"cyan\">Skills</Text>\n\n      {tiers.map((tier) => {\n        const tierSkills = skills.filter((s) => s.tier === tier);\n        const isExpanded = expandedTiers[tier];\n\n        return (\n          <Box key={tier} flexDirection=\"column\" marginTop={1}>\n            <Box>\n              <Text color={TIER_COLORS[tier]} bold>\n                {isExpanded ? \"v\" : \">\"} {TIER_LABELS[tier]}{\" \"}\n              </Text>\n              <Text color=\"gray\">({tierSkills.length})</Text>\n            </Box>\n\n            {isExpanded &&\n              tierSkills.map((skill) => (\n                <SkillRow\n                  key={`${skill.tier}-${skill.name}`}\n                  skill={skill}\n                  onPin={(mode) => onPinSkill(skill, mode)}\n                />\n              ))}\n\n            {isExpanded && tierSkills.length === 0 && (\n              <Text color=\"gray\" dimColor>\n                {\"  \"}No skills found\n              </Text>\n            )}\n          </Box>\n        );\n      })}\n\n      <Box marginTop={1}>\n        <Text color=\"gray\" dimColor>\n          1/2/3 toggle tier\n        </Text>\n      </Box>\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/src/worker/context-manager.ts",
  "content": "import fs from \"node:fs\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\nimport { config } from \"../shared/config.js\";\n\nexport class ContextManager {\n  private cwd: string;\n\n  constructor(cwd: string) {\n    this.cwd = cwd;\n  }\n\n  async createSnapshot(\n    sessionId: string,\n    messages: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>,\n    pins: Array<{ type: string; target: string; mode: string }>\n  ): Promise<{ filePath: string; summary: string }> {\n    const recent = messages.slice(-20);\n    const conversationText = recent\n      .map((m) => `${m.role}: ${m.content.slice(0, 200)}`)\n      .join(\"\\n\");\n\n    const summary = this.buildSummary(conversationText);\n\n    const snapshotDir = config.snapshotDir(this.cwd);\n    fs.mkdirSync(snapshotDir, { recursive: true });\n\n    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, \"-\");\n    const fileName = `snapshot-${timestamp}.md`;\n    const filePath = path.join(snapshotDir, fileName);\n\n    const content = `---\nname: Session Snapshot ${timestamp}\ndescription: Conversation snapshot from clui session ${sessionId}\ntype: project\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n${summary}\n\n## Context State\n\n**Active pins:**\n${pins.map((p) => `- [${p.mode}] ${p.type}: ${p.target}`).join(\"\\n\") || \"None\"}\n\n## Key Messages\n\n${recent.map((m) => `**${m.role}:** ${m.content.slice(0, 500)}`).join(\"\\n\\n\")}\n`;\n\n    fs.writeFileSync(filePath, content, \"utf-8\");\n\n    return { filePath, summary };\n  }\n\n  private buildSummary(conversationText: string): string {\n    const lines = conversationText.split(\"\\n\");\n    const userLines = lines.filter((l) => l.startsWith(\"user:\"));\n\n    if (userLines.length === 0) return \"Empty conversation.\";\n    if (userLines.length === 1) return userLines[0].replace(\"user: \", \"\");\n\n    return `Started with: \"${userLines[0].replace(\"user: \", \"\").slice(0, 100)}\"\\nEnded with: \"${userLines[userLines.length - 1].replace(\"user: \", \"\").slice(0, 100)}\"`;\n  }\n}\n"
}

1	import { connectIPC, sendMessage, socketPath } from "../shared/ipc.js";
2	import type {
3	  ShellToWorkerMessage,
4	  WorkerToShellMessage,
5	  IPCMessage,
6	} from "../shared/ipc.js";
7	import { ClaudeBridge } from "./claude-bridge.js";
8	import type net from "node:net";
9	
10	const workerId = process.argv[2];
11	const cwd = process.argv[3] || process.cwd();
12	
13	if (!workerId) {
14	  console.error("Worker requires workerId as first argument");
15	  process.exit(1);
16	}
17	
18	let socket: net.Socket;
19	let bridge: ClaudeBridge;
20	
21	function send(msg: WorkerToShellMessage): void {
22	  sendMessage(socket, msg);
23	}
24	
25	async function handleMessage(msg: IPCMessage): Promise<void> {
26	  const m = msg as ShellToWorkerMessage;
27	
28	  switch (m.type) {
29	    case "send_prompt":
30	      await bridge.sendPrompt(m.prompt, m.pins);
31	      break;
32	
33	    case "context_reset":
34	      bridge = createBridge();
35	      send({ type: "status_update", data: { contextTokens: 0, contextPercent: 0 } });
36	      break;
37	
38	    case "shutdown":
39	      bridge.interrupt();
40	      socket.end();
41	      process.exit(0);
42	      break;
43	
44	    default:
45	      send({ type: "error", message: `Unknown message type: ${(m as any).type}`, fatal: false });
46	  }
47	}
48	
49	function createBridge(): ClaudeBridge {
50	  return new ClaudeBridge(
51	    {
52	      onStreamChunk: (content) => send({ type: "stream_chunk", content }),
53	      onMessageComplete: (role, content, tokens, cost) =>
54	        send({ type: "message_complete", role, content, tokens, cost }),
55	      onCompacted: (event) => send({ type: "context_compacted", event }),
56	      onStatusUpdate: (data) => send({ type: "status_update", data }),
57	      onError: (message, fatal) => send({ type: "error", message, fatal }),
58	    },
59	    { cwd }
60	  );
61	}
62	
63	async function main(): Promise<void> {
64	  bridge = createBridge();
65	  const sockPath = socketPath(workerId);
66	
67	  try {
68	    socket = await connectIPC(sockPath, handleMessage);
69	    send({ type: "ready", workerId });
70	  } catch (err: any) {
71	    console.error(`Worker ${workerId} failed to connect:`, err.message);
72	    process.exit(1);
73	  }
74	}
75	
76	main().catch((err) => {
77	  console.error("Worker fatal:", err);
78	  process.exit(1);
79	});
80

3746	- [ ] **Step 1: Write worktree manager**
3747	
3748	```typescript
3749	// src/worker/worktree-manager.ts
3750	
3751	import simpleGit, { type SimpleGit } from "simple-git";
3752	import path from "node:path";
3753	
3754	export interface WorktreeInfo {
3755	  path: string;
3756	  branch: string;
3757	}
3758	
3759	export interface DiffStats {
3760	  added: number;
3761	  removed: number;
3762	  modified: number;
3763	}
3764	
3765	export class WorktreeManager {
3766	  private git: SimpleGit;
3767	  private cwd: string;
3768	
3769	  constructor(cwd: string) {
3770	    this.cwd = cwd;
3771	    this.git = simpleGit(cwd);
3772	  }
3773	
3774	  async createWorktree(branch: string): Promise<WorktreeInfo> {
3775	    const worktreePath = path.join(
3776	      path.dirname(this.cwd),
3777	      "worktrees",
3778	      `${path.basename(this.cwd)}-${branch}`
3779	    );
3780	
3781	    // Create branch if it doesn't exist
3782	    try {
3783	      await this.git.branch([branch]);
3784	    } catch {
3785	      // Branch may already exist
3786	    }
3787	
3788	    await this.git.raw(["worktree", "add", worktreePath, branch]);
3789	
3790	    return { path: worktreePath, branch };
3791	  }
3792	
3793	  async removeWorktree(worktreePath: string): Promise<void> {
3794	    await this.git.raw(["worktree", "remove", worktreePath, "--force"]);
3795	  }
3796	
3797	  async getBranch(worktreePath?: string): Promise<string | null> {
3798	    try {
3799	      const git = worktreePath ? simpleGit(worktreePath) : this.git;
3800	      const status = await git.status();
3801	      return status.current;
3802	    } catch {
3803	      return null;
3804	    }
3805	  }
3806	
3807	  async getDiffStats(worktreePath?: string): Promise<DiffStats | null> {
3808	    try {
3809	      const git = worktreePath ? simpleGit(worktreePath) : this.git;
3810	      const diff = await git.diffSummary();
3811	
3812	      return {
3813	        added: diff.insertions,
3814	        removed: diff.deletions,
3815	        modified: diff.files.length,
3816	      };
3817	    } catch {
3818	      return null;
3819	    }
3820	  }
3821	
3822	  async isGitRepo(): Promise<boolean> {
3823	    try {
3824	      await this.git.status();
3825	      return true;
3826	    } catch {
3827	      return false;
3828	    }
3829	  }
3830	}
3831	```

3842	### Task 29: useGit Hook
3843	
3844	**Files:**
3845	- Create: `src/shell/hooks/useGit.ts`
3846	
3847	- [ ] **Step 1: Write git info hook**
3848	
3849	```typescript
3850	// src/shell/hooks/useGit.ts
3851	
3852	import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
3853	import { useStore } from "../store/index.js";
3854	import simpleGit from "simple-git";
3855	
3856	export function useGit(cwd: string): void {
3857	  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);
3858	  const tabs = useStore((s) => s.tabs);
3859	  const updateTabStatus = useStore((s) => s.updateTabStatus);
3860	  const intervalRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
3861	
3862	  useEffect(() => {
3863	    async function updateGitInfo(): Promise<void> {
3864	      if (!activeTabId) return;
3865	
3866	      const activeTab = tabs.find((t) => t.id === activeTabId);
3867	      const gitCwd = activeTab?.worktreePath || cwd;
3868	
3869	      try {
3870	        const git = simpleGit(gitCwd);
3871	        const status = await git.status();
3872	        const diff = await git.diffSummary();
3873	
3874	        updateTabStatus(activeTabId, {
3875	          gitBranch: status.current,
3876	          gitDiffStats: {
3877	            added: diff.insertions,
3878	            removed: diff.deletions,
3879	            modified: diff.files.length,
3880	          },
3881	        });
3882	      } catch {
3883	        updateTabStatus(activeTabId, {
3884	          gitBranch: null,
3885	          gitDiffStats: null,
3886	        });
3887	      }
3888	    }
3889	
3890	    updateGitInfo();
3891	
3892	    // Poll every 5 seconds
3893	    intervalRef.current = setInterval(updateGitInfo, 5000);
3894	
3895	    return () => {
3896	      if (intervalRef.current) clearInterval(intervalRef.current);
3897	    };
3898	  }, [activeTabId, cwd, tabs, updateTabStatus]);
3899	}
3900	```

3930	// src/shell/components/WorktreePrompt.tsx

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/src/shell/components/CompactDetail.tsx",
  "content": "import React, { useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Text, useInput } from \"ink\";\nimport type { CompactEvent } from \"../../shared/types.js\";\n\ninterface CompactDetailProps {\n  events: CompactEvent[];\n}\n\nexport function CompactDetail({ events }: CompactDetailProps): React.ReactElement {\n  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false);\n\n  useInput((input) => {\n    if (input === \"x\") {\n      setExpanded((e) => !e);\n    }\n  });\n\n  if (events.length === 0) return <></>;\n\n  if (!expanded) {\n    return (\n      <Box>\n        <Text color=\"red\">\n          * Context compacted ({events.length}x) -- press 'x' for details\n        </Text>\n      </Box>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"column\" borderStyle=\"single\" borderColor=\"red\" padding={1}>\n      <Text bold color=\"red\">Context Compaction History</Text>\n      {events.map((event, idx) => (\n        <Box key={idx}>\n          <Text color=\"gray\">\n            {new Date(event.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()} --{\" \"}\n            {event.trigger} -- {event.preTokens.toLocaleString()} tokens before\n          </Text>\n        </Box>\n      ))}\n      <Text color=\"gray\" dimColor>Press 'x' to collapse</Text>\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/clui/src/worker/worktree-manager.ts",
  "content": "import simpleGit, { type SimpleGit } from \"simple-git\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\n\nexport interface WorktreeInfo {\n  path: string;\n  branch: string;\n}\n\nexport interface DiffStats {\n  added: number;\n  removed: number;\n  modified: number;\n}\n\nexport class WorktreeManager {\n  private git: SimpleGit;\n  private cwd: string;\n\n  constructor(cwd: string) {\n    this.cwd = cwd;\n    this.git = simpleGit(cwd);\n  }\n\n  async createWorktree(branch: string): Promise<WorktreeInfo> {\n    const worktreePath = path.join(\n      path.dirname(this.cwd),\n      \"worktrees\",\n      `${path.basename(this.cwd)}-${branch}`\n    );\n\n    try {\n      await this.git.branch([branch]);\n    } catch {\n      // Branch may already exist\n    }\n\n    await this.git.raw([\"worktree\", \"add\", worktreePath, branch]);\n\n    return { path: worktreePath, branch };\n  }\n\n  async removeWorktree(worktreePath: string): Promise<void> {\n    await this.git.raw([\"worktree\", \"remove\", worktreePath, \"--force\"]);\n  }\n\n  async getBranch(worktreePath?: string): Promise<string | null> {\n    try {\n      const git = worktreePath ? simpleGit(worktreePath) : this.git;\n      const status = await git.status();\n      return status.current;\n    } catch {\n      return null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  async getDiffStats(worktreePath?: string): Promise<DiffStats | null> {\n    try {\n      const git = worktreePath ? simpleGit(worktreePath) : this.git;\n      const diff = await git.diffSummary();\n\n      return {\n        added: diff.insertions,\n        removed: diff.deletions,\n        modified: diff.files.length,\n      };\n    } catch {\n      return null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  async isGitRepo(): Promise<boolean> {\n    try {\n      await this.git.status();\n      return true;\n    } catch {\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import React from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Text, useInput } from \"ink\";\n\ninterface WorktreePromptProps {\n  tabName: string;\n  worktreePath: string;\n  onKeep: () => void;\n  onCleanup: () => void;\n  onCancel: () => void;\n}\n\nexport function WorktreePrompt({\n  tabName,\n  worktreePath,\n  onKeep,\n  onCleanup,\n  onCancel,\n}: WorktreePromptProps): React.ReactElement {\n  useInput((input, key) => {\n    if (input === \"k\") onKeep();\n    if (input === \"c\") onCleanup();\n    if (key.escape) onCancel();\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <Box\n      flexDirection=\"column\"\n      borderStyle=\"double\"\n      borderColor=\"yellow\"\n      padding={1}\n    >\n      <Text bold color=\"yellow\">\n        Close tab \"{tabName}\"?\n      </Text>\n      <Text color=\"gray\">\n        This tab has a worktree at: {worktreePath}\n      </Text>\n      <Box marginTop={1} flexDirection=\"column\">\n        <Text>\n          <Text color=\"green\" bold>k</Text> -- Keep worktree, close tab\n        </Text>\n        <Text>\n          <Text color=\"red\" bold>c</Text> -- Cleanup worktree and close tab\n        </Text>\n        <Text>\n          <Text color=\"gray\" bold>Esc</Text> -- Cancel\n        </Text>\n      </Box>\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

All 6 phases implemented. Here's the summary:

**30 files created**, build passes, 14 tests pass.

### What's built:

| Phase | Status | What |
|-------|--------|------|
| 1 - Foundation | Done | Scaffold, types, config, SQLite, IPC protocol |
| 2 - Multi-Tab | Done | Worker processes, IPC server, useWorkerManager |
| 3 - Sessions | Done | Sessions store, useSessions hook, SessionSidebar |
| 4 - Skills | Done | Skills store, 3-tier discovery, SkillsSidebar, pins, ContextBar |
| 5 - Context | Done | Snapshot, compact indicator, context reset, cross-tab reading |
| 6 - Worktrees | Done | WorktreeManager, useGit, WorktreePrompt |

### Key hotkeys:
- `Ctrl+T` new tab, `Ctrl+W` close, `Tab` cycle, `Ctrl+1-9` switch
- `Ctrl+H` toggle sessions sidebar, `Ctrl+S` toggle skills sidebar
- `Ctrl+R` context reset, `Ctrl+Shift+S` snapshot
- `@file` and `/skill` in input for quick pinning

### To run:
```bash
npm run build && node dist/index.js
```

The Claude Agent SDK bridge is wired up — each tab spawns an isolated worker process communicating over Unix Domain Sockets. The SDK API shape (`query`, `SDKMessage`, `listSessions`) may need adjustment once you verify the exact SDK exports at runtime.

> AGENT

The problem: `spawnWorker` is called, which eventually triggers `setWorkerReady` updating `tabs`, which re-triggers the effect. The `!tab.workerId && !tab.workerReady` check should prevent re-spawn, but `spawnWorker` doesn't immediately set `workerId` on the tab — it happens async via IPC `ready` message. So it keeps spawning.

Fix: track which tabs we've already spawned for using a ref:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit

```json

  "new_string": "import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { Box, useInput } from \"ink\";"
}

The `getPins` and `filteredSessions` methods return new arrays each call, which breaks Zustand's `useStore` selector (reference equality). Fix: read `pins` directly instead of calling methods in selectors.

> AGENT

The problem selectors are ones that derive new arrays/objects every call:

- `s.pins[s.activeTabId] || []` — the `|| []` creates a new empty array each time
- `s.tabMessages[activeTabId] || []` — same
- `s.tabStreamBuffer[activeTabId] || ""` — string is fine (primitive)
- `s.tabCompactEvents[activeTabId] || []` — same new array issue
- `s.tabStatus[activeTabId]` with nullish fallback

The root cause: when `activeTabId` exists but the key isn't in the record yet, `|| []` returns a new array reference every render, triggering infinite re-renders.

```

> AGENT

The core problem: `activeTabId` is used as a closure variable inside selectors, but it's a React value, not the store value. So the selector closes over `activeTabId` from the component scope — but the selector function reference changes every render, causing Zustand to re-subscribe repeatedly.

The fix: read `activeTabId` from the store state `s` inside the selector, not from the closure. But wait — they already do that in some places and not others. Let me look at ChatView more carefully: it uses `activeTabId` from a *separate* `useStore` call as a closure, which means the selector function changes on every render since `activeTabId` changes → re-subscribe → re-render → loop.

The real fix: use `useShallow` or restructure to not use closures in selectors. Simplest: use a single store read per component.

> TOOL

  "content": "import React from \"react\";\nimport { Box, Static, Text } from \"ink\";\nimport { useStore } from \"../store/index.js\";\nimport type { Message } from \"../../shared/types.js\";\n\nfunction MessageBubble({ msg }: { msg: Message }): React.ReactElement {\n  const color = msg.role === \"user\" ? \"green\" : msg.role === \"assistant\" ? \"white\" : \"gray\";\n  const prefix = msg.role === \"user\" ? \"> \" : \"  \";\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"column\" marginBottom={1}>\n      <Text color={color} dimColor={msg.role === \"system\"}>\n        {prefix}\n        {msg.content}\n      </Text>\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n\nconst EMPTY_MESSAGES: Message[] = [];\n\nexport function ChatView(): React.ReactElement {\n  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);\n  const allMessages = useStore((s) => s.tabMessages);\n  const allStreamBuffers = useStore((s) => s.tabStreamBuffer);\n\n  const messages = (activeTabId && allMessages[activeTabId]) || EMPTY_MESSAGES;\n  const streamBuffer = (activeTabId && allStreamBuffers[activeTabId]) || \"\";\n\n  return (\n    <Box flexDirection=\"column\" flexGrow={1} paddingX={1}>\n      <Static items={messages}>\n        {(msg) => <MessageBubble key={msg.id} msg={msg} />}\n      </Static>\n\n      {streamBuffer.length > 0 && (\n        <Box>\n          <Text color=\"white\">  {streamBuffer}</Text>\n          <Text color=\"cyan\">|</Text>\n        </Box>\n      )}\n\n      {messages.length === 0 && streamBuffer.length === 0 && (\n        <Box justifyContent=\"center\" alignItems=\"center\" flexGrow={1}>\n          <Text color=\"gray\">Start a conversation...</Text>\n        </Box>\n      )}\n    </Box>\n  );\n}\n"
}

5	export function useGit(cwd: string) {
6	  const activeTabId = useStore((s) => s.activeTabId);
7	  const updateTabStatus = useStore((s) => s.updateTabStatus);
8	
9	  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
10	    if (!activeTabId) return;
11	
12	    try {
13	      const git = simpleGit(cwd);
14	      const isRepo = await git.checkIsRepo();
15	      if (!isRepo) {
16	        updateTabStatus(activeTabId, { gitBranch: null, gitDiffStats: null });
17	        return;
18	      }
19	
20	      const branch = await git.revparse(["--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"]);
21	      const diffSummary = await git.diffSummary();
22	
23	      updateTabStatus(activeTabId, {
24	        gitBranch: branch.trim(),
25	        gitDiffStats: {
26	          added: diffSummary.insertions,
27	          removed: diffSummary.deletions,
28	          modified: diffSummary.changed,
29	        },
30	      });
31	    } catch {
32	      updateTabStatus(activeTabId, { gitBranch: null, gitDiffStats: null });
33	    }
34	  }, [cwd, activeTabId, updateTabStatus]);
35	
36	  useEffect(() => {
37	    refresh();
38	    const interval = setInterval(refresh, 10000);
39	    return () => clearInterval(interval);
40	  }, [refresh]);
41	
42	  return { refresh };
43	}

> AGENT

`useGit` has `updateTabStatus` in its dependency array. `updateTabStatus` is a store function — its reference is stable. But `activeTabId` changes → new `refresh` callback → effect re-runs → calls `updateTabStatus` → updates `tabStatus` → but that shouldn't change `activeTabId`. This should be fine.

6	async function loadClaudeSessions(): Promise<Session[]> {

9	    const listSessions = (sdk as any).listSessions;

12	
13	    return sdkSessions.map((s: any) => ({
14	      id: s.sessionId || s.id,

16	      name: s.title || s.name || `Session ${(s.sessionId || s.id)?.slice(0, 8)}`,
17	      color: null,

34	export function useSessions(): {
35	  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
36	} {

38	
39	  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
40	    const [sdkSessions, dbSessions] = await Promise.all([
41	      loadClaudeSessions(),
42	      Promise.resolve(db.listSessions(100)),
43	    ]);
44	
45	    const dbMap = new Map(dbSessions.map((s) => [s.claudeSessionId || s.id, s]));
46	
47	    const merged: Session[] = [];
48	    const seen = new Set<string>();
49	
50	    for (const sdkSession of sdkSessions) {
51	      const dbOverride = dbMap.get(sdkSession.id);
52	      if (dbOverride) {
53	        merged.push({
54	          ...sdkSession,
55	          name: dbOverride.name || sdkSession.name,
56	          color: dbOverride.color || sdkSession.color,
57	        });
58	      } else {
59	        merged.push(sdkSession);
60	      }
61	      seen.add(sdkSession.id);
62	    }
63	
64	    for (const dbSession of dbSessions) {
65	      if (!seen.has(dbSession.claudeSessionId || dbSession.id)) {
66	        merged.push(dbSession);
67	      }
68	    }
69	
70	    merged.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt - a.updatedAt);
71	    setSessions(merged);
72	  }, [setSessions]);
73	
74	  useEffect(() => {
75	    refresh();
76	  }, [refresh]);
77	
78	  return { refresh };
79	}
